d) loss or substantial weakening of the function of a sensory organ,

e) damage to an important organ,

g) inducement of miscarriage or killing of a foetus,

i) long-lasting impairment of health. Mental disorder

By mental disorder is meant, in addition to mental disorders resulting from mental illness, also profound impairment of consciousness, mental retardation, serious asocial personality disorder or other serious mental or sexual deviation. State-imposed and recognised duty of confidentiality

By a state-imposed or recognised duty of confidentiality is considered confidentiality which is imposed or recognised by another legal regulation. A duty of confidentiality whose scope is not defined by another legal regulation but follows from a legal act made on the basis of another legal regulation is not considered, according to the criminal law, to be a state-recognised duty of confidentiality. Close person

By close person is meant a relative in direct line, adopter, adoptee, sibling, spouse and partner; other persons in a family relationship or similar relationship are considered close to each other only if the harm suffered by one of them would reasonably be felt by the other as his own harm. Child

By child is meant a person under eighteen years of age, unless the criminal law provides otherwise. Official person

c) the President of the Czech Republic, a deputy or senator of the Parliament of the Czech Republic, a member of the Government of the Czech Republic or another person holding a position in another public authority,

d) a member of a council or a responsible official of territorial self-government, a state administration body or another public authority,

e) a member of the armed forces or a security corps or a municipal police officer,

f) a judicial executor in the performance of execution activity and in activities performed by commission of a court or public prosecutor,

g) a notary when performing acts in inheritance proceedings as a court commissioner,

h) a financial arbitrator and his deputy,

i) a natural person who has been appointed as a forest guard, nature guard, hunting guard or fishing guard, insofar as he fulfils tasks of the state or society and uses the entrusted powers for the fulfilment of these tasks. (2) For criminal liability and protection of an official person it is required according to individual provisions of the criminal law that the offence was committed in connection with his powers and responsibilities. (3) An official person of a foreign state or international organisation is considered, under the conditions specified in paragraphs 1 and 2, to be an official person under the criminal law if so provided by an international treaty or if he acts on the territory of the Czech Republic with the consent of the authorities of the Czech Republic; this consent is not required if it concerns an official person of the international criminal court, international criminal tribunal or a similar international judicial body which meets at least one of the conditions specified in § 145(1)(a) of the Act on International Judicial Cooperation in Criminal Matters. Public prosecutor

By public prosecutor is also meant a European Delegated Prosecutor, European Prosecutor and European Chief Prosecutor within the scope of their competence established by a directly applicable regulation of the European Union governing the establishment of the European Public Prosecutor's Office. Insolvency administrator and insolvency proceedings

(1) By insolvency administrator is also meant a preliminary insolvency administrator, deputy of the insolvency administrator, separate insolvency administrator, special insolvency administrator; the position of insolvency administrator is also held by a bankruptcy estate administrator, preliminary bankruptcy estate administrator, special bankruptcy estate administrator, deputy of the bankruptcy estate administrator and composition administrator. By insolvency administrator is also meant a person whom the insolvency administrator has designated according to another legal regulation to represent him in the exercise of his powers established by another legal regulation on the territory of another state, further a foreign insolvency administrator, foreign insolvency administrator of an insurance company or reinsurance company and a person whom the foreign insolvency administrator or foreign insolvency administrator of an insurance company or reinsurance company has appointed according to another legal regulation to assist him or represent him. (2) By insolvency proceedings is also meant bankruptcy and composition proceedings. Organised criminal group

An organised criminal group is an association of at least three criminally responsible persons with an internal organisational structure, with division of functions and division of activities, which is focused on the systematic commission of intentional criminal activity. Terrorist group

(1) A terrorist group is an association of at least three criminally responsible persons which has a more lasting character, in which a division of activities among its individual members is carried out, its activity is characterised by planning and coordination and is focused on committing the offence of high treason committed in the form of a terrorist attack or terror (§ 309), the offence of terrorist attack (§ 311) or the offence of terror (§ 312) (hereinafter "terrorist offence"). (2) The fulfilment of the characteristics of a terrorist group does not exclude the application of provisions of this and other laws on an organised group and an organised criminal group; the provisions of § 361 and 363 shall not apply. Addictive substance

By addictive substance is meant alcohol, narcotic drugs, psychotropic substances and other substances capable of adversely affecting a person's psyche or his control or recognition abilities or social behaviour. Public document

(1) By public document is meant a document issued by a court of the Czech Republic, another public authority or another entity commissioned or authorised to do so by another legal regulation within the limits of its powers, certifying that it is an order or declaration of the body or other entity that issued the document, or certifying a legally significant fact. A public document is also a document declared to be public by another legal regulation. (2) Protection under § 348 is also provided to a public document issued by a public authority or another entity authorised to do so of a foreign state or by a body of an international organisation, if it has effect on the territory of the Czech Republic according to an international treaty. Public utility facility

By public utility facility is meant a public protective facility against fire, flood or other natural disaster, defensive or protective facility against air and other similar attacks or their consequences, protective facility against leakage of pollutants, energy or water supply facility, submarine cable or submarine pipeline, electronic communications equipment and networks and terminal telecommunications and radio equipment, equipment of a postal licence holder, public transport facility, including components of a railway and railway vehicles in public railway transport and vertical prohibitory or mandatory traffic signs and traffic signs regulating priority. Dwelling

By dwelling is meant a house, flat or other premises serving for habitation and appurtenances belonging to them. Thing

By thing is also meant controllable natural force. Provisions on things also extend to live animals and processed separated parts of the human body, unless something else follows from individual provisions of the criminal law. Thing belonging to the offender

A thing belongs to the offender if he owns it at the time of the decision on it, it is part of his property or he factually deals with it as the owner without the rightful owner or holder of such thing being known. Instrument of criminal activity

By instrument of criminal activity is meant a thing which was destined or used for the commission of an offence, including fruits and revenues. Proceeds of criminal activity

(1) By proceeds of criminal activity is meant any economic advantage originating from an offence. (2) By direct proceeds of criminal activity is meant a thing which was obtained by an offence or as a reward for it, including fruits and revenues. (3) By indirect proceeds of criminal activity is meant a thing, including fruits and revenues,

a) which was acquired, even only in part, for a thing constituting direct proceeds of criminal activity,

b) into which a thing constituting direct proceeds of criminal activity was, even only in part, transformed, or

c) whose appreciation occurred, even only in part, through a thing constituting direct proceeds of criminal activity. Writings

By writings is meant data, audio and image records, depictions and other representations, unless something else follows from individual provisions of the criminal law. Computer system

By computer system is meant a device or group of interconnected or associated devices, one or more of which performs automatic data processing on the basis of a program. By computer system is also meant data stored, processed, retrieved again or transmitted by this device or group of devices for the purpose of its or their operation, use, protection and maintenance. Determination of the amount of damage

In determining the amount of damage, the basis is the price at which the thing which was the subject of the attack is usually sold at the time and place of the act. If the amount of damage cannot be determined in this way, the basis is the purposefully incurred costs of procuring the same or similar thing or restoring the thing to its previous state. Limits of the amount of damage, benefit, costs for remedying environmental damage and value of a thing

(1) For the purposes of this Act is meant

a) by damage not insignificant damage reaching the amount of at least CZK 10,000,

b) by damage not small damage reaching the amount of at least CZK 50,000,

c) by greater damage damage reaching the amount of at least CZK 100,000,

d) by substantial damage damage reaching the amount of at least CZK 1,000,000 and

e) by damage of large extent damage reaching the amount of at least CZK 10,000,000. (2) The amounts specified in paragraph 1 shall be applied mutatis mutandis for determining the amount of benefit, costs for remedying the consequences of environmental damage and value of a thing. Calculation of time

Where this Act associates some effect with the lapse of a certain period, the day on which the event determining its beginning occurred is not counted in it. SPECIAL PART

OFFENCES AGAINST LIFE AND HEALTH

Offences against life

Murder

(1) Whoever intentionally kills another shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to eighteen years. (2) Whoever intentionally kills another with premeditation or after prior deliberation shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

c) on a child under fifteen years of age,

d) on an official person in the performance or for the performance of his powers,

e) on a witness, expert or interpreter in connection with the performance of their duty,

f) on a healthcare worker in the performance of a healthcare occupation or profession aimed at saving life or protecting health, or on another who was fulfilling his similar duty in the protection of life, health or property arising from his occupation, profession, position or function or imposed on him according to the law,

g) on another for his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion,

i) in a particularly cruel or torturous manner, or

j) with the intention of obtaining a financial benefit for himself or another or in an effort to cover up or facilitate another offence or from another reprehensible motive. Manslaughter

(1) Whoever intentionally kills another in a state of strong agitation from fear, fright, confusion or other excusable emotion or as a result of prior reprehensible conduct of the injured person shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

c) on a child under fifteen years of age. Infanticide by mother

A mother who in agitation caused by childbirth intentionally kills her newborn child during childbirth or immediately after it shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. Causing death by negligence

(1) Whoever causes the death of another by negligence shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 because he breached an important duty arising from his occupation, profession, position or function or imposed on him according to the law. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 because he grossly violated laws on environmental protection or laws on work or traffic safety or hygiene laws. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he causes the death of at least two persons by the act referred to in paragraph 3. Participation in suicide

(1) Whoever induces another to commit suicide or assists another in committing suicide shall be punished, if at least an attempt at suicide occurred, by imprisonment for up to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a child or on a pregnant woman. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a child under fifteen years of age or on a person suffering from a mental disorder. Offences against health

Grievous bodily harm

(1) Whoever intentionally causes serious bodily harm to another shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

c) on a child under fifteen years of age,

d) on a witness, expert or interpreter in connection with the performance of their duty,

e) on a healthcare worker in the performance of a healthcare occupation or profession aimed at saving life or protecting health, or on another who was fulfilling his similar duty in the protection of life, health or property arising from his occupation, profession, position or function or imposed on him according to the law,

f) on another for his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion,

g) repeatedly or after he committed another particularly serious felony connected with intentionally causing serious bodily harm or death or its attempt, or

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Bodily harm

(1) Whoever intentionally harms the health of another shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

b) on a child under fifteen years of age,

c) on a witness, expert or interpreter in connection with the performance of their duty,

d) on a healthcare worker in the performance of a healthcare occupation or profession aimed at saving life or protecting health, or on another who was fulfilling his similar duty in the protection of life, health or property arising from his occupation, profession, position or function or imposed on him according to the law, or

e) on another for his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he causes serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Bodily harm from excusable motive

(1) Whoever intentionally causes bodily harm to another in a state of strong agitation from fear, fright, confusion or other excusable emotion or as a result of prior reprehensible conduct of the injured person shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if he causes serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) Whoever intentionally causes serious bodily harm to another in a state of strong agitation from fear, fright, confusion or other excusable emotion or as a result of prior reprehensible conduct of the injured person shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 3 on two or more persons,

b) he commits such an act on a pregnant woman, or

c) he commits such an act on a child under fifteen years of age. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 3. Grievous bodily harm by negligence

(1) Whoever causes serious bodily harm to another by negligence shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years or by a pecuniary penalty if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 because he breached an important duty arising from his occupation, profession, position or function or imposed on him according to the law. (3) Whoever causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons by negligence because he grossly violated laws on environmental protection or laws on work or traffic safety or hygiene laws shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years. Bodily harm by negligence

(1) Whoever harms the health of another by negligence by breaching an important duty arising from his occupation, profession, position or function or imposed on him according to the law shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever causes bodily harm to at least two persons by negligence because he grossly violated laws on environmental protection or laws on work or traffic safety or hygiene laws shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years. Offences endangering life or health

Torture and other inhuman and cruel treatment

(1) Whoever causes physical or mental suffering to another by torture or other inhuman and cruel treatment in connection with the exercise of powers of a state administration body, territorial self-government, court or other public authority shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as an official person,

b) he commits such an act on a witness, expert or interpreter in connection with the performance of their duty,

c) he commits such an act on another for his actual or supposed race, membership of an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion,

d) he commits such an act together with at least two persons, or

e) he commits such an act repeatedly.(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 against a pregnant woman,

b) if they commit such an act against a child younger than fifteen years,

c) if they commit such an act in a particularly cruel or tormenting manner, or

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to eighteen years if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Failure to Provide Assistance

(1) Whoever fails to provide necessary assistance to a person who is in danger of death or shows signs of serious health disorder or other serious illness, although they can do so without danger to themselves or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever fails to provide necessary assistance to a person who is in danger of death or shows signs of serious health disorder or serious illness, although they are obliged to provide such assistance according to the nature of their employment, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity.

Failure to Provide Assistance by a Driver of a Vehicle

A driver of a vehicle who, after a traffic accident in which they participated, fails to provide necessary assistance to a person who suffered harm to health in the accident, although they can do so without danger to themselves or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years or prohibition of activity.

Spreading Contagious Human Disease

(1) Whoever intentionally causes or increases the danger of introduction or spreading of a contagious disease in humans shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property,

c) if by such an act they violate an important duty arising from their employment, occupation, position or function or imposed on them by law, or

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they cause serious harm to health to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they cause death to at least two persons by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Spreading Contagious Human Disease by Negligence

(1) Whoever by negligence causes or increases the danger of introduction or spreading of a contagious disease in humans shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property,

b) if by such an act they violate an important duty arising from their employment, occupation, position or function or imposed on them by law, or

c) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years,

a) if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they cause serious harm to health by the act specified in paragraph 2 letter b).

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 3 because they grossly violated laws for the protection of public health.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they cause death to at least two persons by the act specified in paragraph 1 because they grossly violated regulations for the protection of public health.

Common Provision

The Government shall establish by regulation what is considered a contagious human disease.

Endangerment by Venereal Disease

Whoever exposes another, even by negligence, to the danger of infection with a venereal disease shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

Endangering Health with Defective Food and Other Items

(1) Whoever, in violation of another legal regulation, has for sale or manufactures for this purpose or obtains for themselves or another intentionally food or other items whose consumption or use for the usual purpose is dangerous to human health shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property,

c) if by such an act they violate an important duty arising from their employment, occupation, position or function or imposed on them by law, or

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they cause serious harm to health to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they cause death to at least two persons by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Endangering Health with Defective Food and Other Items by Negligence

(1) Whoever, in violation of another legal regulation, has for sale or manufactures for this purpose or obtains for themselves or another by negligence food or other items whose consumption or use for the usual purpose is dangerous to human health shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property,

b) if by such an act they violate an important duty arising from their employment, occupation, position or function or imposed on them by law, or

c) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years,

a) if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they cause serious harm to health by the act specified in paragraph 2 letter b).

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 3 letter a) because they grossly violated hygienic or other laws concerning such food or items.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they cause death to at least two persons by the act specified in paragraph 1 because they grossly violated hygienic or other laws concerning such food or items.

Brawl

(1) Whoever intentionally endangers the life or health of another by participating in a brawl shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or a fine if serious harm to health is caused to another during the act specified in paragraph 1.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if death is caused to another during the act specified in paragraph 1.

Criminal Offences Against Pregnancy of a Woman

Unlawful Termination of Pregnancy Without Consent of the Pregnant Woman

(1) Whoever artificially terminates the pregnancy of a pregnant woman without her consent shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 against a woman younger than eighteen years,

b) if they commit such an act using violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm,

c) if they commit such an act by abusing the distress or dependence of the pregnant woman,

d) if they commit such an act repeatedly, or

e) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they cause serious harm to health to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years if they cause death to at least two persons by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Unlawful Termination of Pregnancy With Consent of the Pregnant Woman

(1) Whoever, with the consent of a pregnant woman, artificially terminates her pregnancy other than in a manner permissible under the law on artificial termination of pregnancy shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or prohibition of activity.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 against a woman younger than eighteen years,

b) if they obtain for themselves or another substantial benefit by such an act,

c) if they commit such an act systematically, or

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they cause serious harm to health to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they cause death to at least two persons by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Assistance to a Pregnant Woman in Artificial Termination of Pregnancy

(1) Whoever assists a pregnant woman in order that

a) she herself artificially terminates her pregnancy, or

b) she requests another or permits another to artificially terminate her pregnancy other than in a manner permissible under the law on artificial termination of pregnancy,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 against a woman younger than eighteen years, or

b) if by such an act they contribute to serious harm to health of the pregnant woman.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years if by the act specified in paragraph 1 they contribute to the death of the pregnant woman.

Inducing a Pregnant Woman to Artificial Termination of Pregnancy

(1) Whoever induces a pregnant woman in order that

a) she herself artificially terminates her pregnancy, or

b) she requests another or permits another to artificially terminate her pregnancy other than in a manner permissible under the law on artificial termination of pregnancy,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 against a woman younger than eighteen years,

b) if they commit such an act by abusing the distress or dependence of the pregnant woman, or

c) if by such an act they contribute to serious harm to health of the pregnant woman.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years if by the act specified in paragraph 1 they contribute to the death of the pregnant woman.

Common Provision

A pregnant woman who herself artificially terminates her pregnancy or requests another to do so or permits them to do so is not criminally liable for such an act, not even under provisions on instigator and accomplice.

Criminal Offences Related to Unauthorized Handling of Human Tissues and Organs, Human Embryo and Human Genome

Unauthorized Removal of Tissues and Organs

(1) Whoever, in violation of another legal regulation, performs removal of tissue, cell or organ from another's body shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, in violation of another legal regulation, obtains, procures, offers, imports, exports or transports for themselves or another removed human tissue, cell or removed human organ from the body of a living person, or otherwise handles such tissue, cell or organ.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 against a child,

b) if they commit such an act using violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm,

c) if they commit such an act by abusing distress or dependence,

d) if they commit such an act against at least two persons,

e) if they commit such an act repeatedly,

f) if they commit such an act as a member of an organized group,

g) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act, or

h) if they obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another by such an act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 against a child younger than fifteen years,

b) if they commit such an act in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states,

c) if they cause death by such an act, or

d) if they obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another by such an act.

Unlawful Handling of Tissues and Organs

(1) Whoever, in violation of another legal regulation, performs removal of tissue, cell or organ from the body of a dead person shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever handles removed human tissue, cell or removed human organ in violation of another legal regulation with the intent to enrich themselves or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 repeatedly,

b) if they commit such an act as a member of an organized group, or

c) if they obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another by such an act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another by the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if they commit such an act in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states.

Removal of Tissue, Organ and Performance of Transplantation for Remuneration

(1) Whoever, in violation of another legal regulation, offers, promises or provides to another or for another remuneration for removal of tissue or organ from their body or for performance of transplantation shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, themselves or through another, in connection with removal of tissue or organ or with performance of transplantation, requests, receives or has promised for themselves or another remuneration in violation of another legal regulation.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if serious harm to health occurs in connection with such an act, or

c) if such an act is committed against a child.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states,

b) if death occurs in connection with such an act, or

c) if they commit such an act against a child younger than fifteen years.

Unlawful Handling of Human Embryo and Human Genome

(1) Whoever, in violation of another legal regulation,

a) uses for research a human embryo or a larger quantity of human embryonic stem cells or their lines,

b) imports or exports a human embryo or a larger quantity of human embryonic stem cells or their lines, or

c) transfers a human genome into cells of another animal species or vice versa,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity.

a) whoever performs interventions aimed at creating a human embryo for a purpose other than transfer into a female organism,

b) whoever transfers a created human embryo into the womb of another animal species, or

c) whoever during research on human embryonic stem cells performs manipulations with these cells aimed at creating a new human individual (reproductive cloning).

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act repeatedly, or

c) if they obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another by such an act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states, or

b) if they obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another by such an act.

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST FREEDOM AND RIGHTS TO PROTECTION OF PERSONALITY, PRIVACY AND SECRECY OF CORRESPONDENCE

Criminal Offences Against Freedom

Trafficking in Human Beings

(1) Whoever induces, procures, hires, lures, seduces, transports, conceals, detains, receives or hands over a child in order that the child be used

a) by another for sexual intercourse or other forms of sexual abuse or harassment or for production of pornographic work,

b) by another for removal of tissue, cell or organ from their body,

c) for service in armed forces,

d) for slavery or serfdom, or

e) for forced labour or other forms of exploitation,

or whoever profits from such conduct, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, by using violence, threat of violence or other serious harm or deception or by abusing their error, distress or dependence, induces, procures, hires, lures, seduces, transports, conceals, detains, receives or hands over a person other than specified in paragraph 1 in order that they be used

a) by another for sexual intercourse or other forms of sexual abuse or harassment or for production of pornographic work,

b) by another for removal of tissue, cell or organ from their body,

c) for service in armed forces,

d) for slavery or serfdom, or

e) for forced labour or other forms of exploitation,

or whoever profits from such conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if by such an act they expose another to danger of serious harm to health or death,

c) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another, or

d) if they commit such an act with the intent that another be used for prostitution.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they cause serious harm to health by the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2,

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another, or

c) if they commit such an act in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to eighteen years or forfeiture of property if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2.

Entrusting a Child into the Power of Another

(1) Whoever for remuneration entrusts a child into the power of another for the purpose of adoption or for another similar purpose shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act,

c) if they commit such an act repeatedly, or

d) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or forfeiture of property,

a) if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1,

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another, or

c) if they commit such an act in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states.

Deprivation of Personal Freedom

(1) Whoever without authorization imprisons another or otherwise deprives them of personal freedom shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act against another for their actual or presumed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political belief, religion or because they are actually or presumably without religion,

c) if they cause physical or psychological suffering by such an act,

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act, or

e) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years,

a) if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

Restriction of Personal Freedom

(1) Whoever without authorization prevents another from using personal freedom shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 with the intent to facilitate another criminal offence.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act against another for their actual or presumed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political belief, religion or because they are actually or presumably without religion,

c) if they cause physical or psychological suffering by such an act,

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act, or

e) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years,

a) if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

Abduction

(1) Whoever by deception, violence or threat of violence or other harm abducts another into the Czech Republic or into another state, or induces them to go to the Czech Republic or to another state, or prevents them from returning from the Czech Republic or from another state, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act against another for their actual or presumed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political belief, religion or because they are actually or presumably without religion,

c) if they cause physical or psychological suffering by such an act,

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act, or

e) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years,

a) if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

Robbery

(1) Whoever uses violence or threat of imminent violence against another with the intent to seize another's property shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act,

c) if they cause substantial damage by such an act, or

d) if they commit such an act with the intent to enable or facilitate commission of a terrorist criminal offence, criminal offence of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (§ 312f).

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if they cause damage of large scale by the act specified in paragraph 1.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to eighteen years if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Hostage Taking

(1) Whoever seizes a hostage and threatens to kill them or cause them harm to health or other serious harm, with the aim of forcing another to do, omit or tolerate something, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if a child is a hostage in such an act,

c) if multiple persons are hostages in such an act,

d) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act, or

e) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to eighteen years if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Extortion

(1) Whoever by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm forces another to do, omit or tolerate something shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years or a fine.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act with at least two persons,

c) if they commit such an act with a weapon,

d) if they cause substantial damage by such an act,

e) if they commit such an act against a pregnant woman,

f) if they commit such an act against a witness, expert or interpreter in connection with the performance of their duty, or

g) if they commit such an act against another for their actual or presumed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political belief, religion or because they are actually or presumably without religion.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years,

a) if they cause serious harm to health by such an act,

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to enable or facilitate commission of a terrorist criminal offence, criminal offence of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offence (§ 312f), or

c) if they cause damage of large scale by such an act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years if they cause death by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Restriction of Freedom of Religion

(1) Whoever by violence, threat of violence or threat of other harm

a) forces another to participate in a religious ceremony,

b) without authorization prevents another from such participation, or

c) otherwise prevents another from using freedom of religion,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or a fine if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) against at least three persons, or

Oppression

(1) Whoever forces another, by abusing their distress or dependence, to do, omit or tolerate something shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or prohibition of activity.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years,

a) if they cause substantial damage by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or a fine,

a) if they cause damage of large scale by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

Violation of Privacy of the Home

(1) Whoever unlawfully enters another's dwelling or unlawfully remains there shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they use violence or threat of imminent violence during the act specified in paragraph 1 or overcome an obstacle whose purpose is to prevent entry.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or a fine if they use violence or threat of imminent violence during the act specified in paragraph 1 and commit such an act with a weapon or with at least two persons.

Violation of Freedom of Association and Assembly

(1) Whoever restricts another by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm in the exercise of their right of association or assembly shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity.

(2) Whoever, in connection with an assembly subject to notification obligation, by violence or threat of imminent violence opposes the orderly measures of the convener or designated organizers of such assembly shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

Criminal Offences Against Rights to Protection of Personality, Privacy and Secrecy of Correspondence

Unauthorized Handling of Personal Data

(1) Whoever, even by negligence, unlawfully publishes, communicates, makes accessible, otherwise processes or appropriates personal data that were collected about another in connection with the exercise of public authority, and thereby causes serious harm to the rights or legitimate interests of the person to whom the personal data relate, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or prohibition of activity.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, even by negligence, violates the duty of confidentiality imposed or recognized by the state by unlawfully publishing, communicating or making accessible to a third person personal data obtained in connection with the exercise of their occupation, employment or function, and thereby causes serious harm to the rights or legitimate interests of the person to whom the personal data relate.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years, a fine or prohibition of activity,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act by press, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means,

c) if they cause substantial damage by such an act, or

d) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if they cause damage of large scale by the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

Violation of the Rights of Others

(1) Whoever causes serious harm to the rights of another by shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years,

a) if they cause substantial harm to the rights of another by the act specified in paragraph 1,

b) if they obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another by such an act, or

c) if during such an act they present themselves as an official.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years,

a) if they cause harm to the rights of another of large scale by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) if they obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another by such an act.

Violation of Secrecy of Transmitted Messages

(1) Whoever intentionally violates the secrecy of

a) a sealed letter or other document during the provision of postal service or transported by another transport service or transport facility,

b) a data, text, voice, sound or image message sent via an electronic communications network and attributable to an identified subscriber or user who receives the message, or

c) non-public transmission of data to a computer system, from it or within it, including electromagnetic emissions from a computer system transmitting such data,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activity.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, with the intent to cause damage to another or obtain unauthorized benefit for themselves or another,

a) discloses a secret about which they learned from a document, telegram, telephone conversation or transmission via an electronic communications network that was not intended for them, or

b) uses such a secret.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or prohibition of activity,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if they commit such an act from a reprehensible motive,

c) if they cause substantial damage by such an act, or

d) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for themselves or another.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or a fine,

a) if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as an official,

b) if they cause damage of large scale by such an act, or

c) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

(5) An employee of an operator of postal services, telecommunication service or computer system or anyone else performing communication activities who

a) commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2,

b) intentionally enables another to commit such an act, or

c) alters or suppresses a document contained in a postal item or transported by a transport facility or a message sent by non-public data transmission, by telephone, telegraph or other similar means,

shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years, a fine or prohibition of activity.

(6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years,

a) if they cause damage of large scale by the act specified in paragraph 5, or

b) if they commit such an act with the intent to obtain benefit of large scale for themselves or another.

Violation of Secrecy of Documents and Other Records Kept in Private

(1) Whoever unlawfully violates the secrecy of a document or other writing, photograph, film or other recording, data stored in a computer system or on an information carrier or other document kept in private of another by publishing it, making it accessible to a third person or using it in another manner shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of a thing if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 with the intent to obtain for themselves or another property or other benefit, cause damage or other serious harm to another, or threaten their social reputation.(3) By imprisonment for six months to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he commits such act against another for his actual or supposed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion,

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act, or

d) if he commits such act with intent to obtain substantial benefit for himself or another.

(4) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another.

Defamation

(1) Whoever communicates false information about another that is capable of substantially endangering his reputation among fellow citizens, in particular causing him harm in employment, disturbing his family relations or causing him other serious harm, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) By imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activities, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means.

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST HUMAN DIGNITY IN THE SEXUAL SPHERE

Rape

(1) Whoever compels another by violence or threat of violence or threat of other serious harm to sexual intercourse, or whoever abuses his defenselessness for such act, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for two years to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by coitus or other sexual intercourse performed in a manner comparable to coitus,

(3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a child under fifteen years of age,

b) if he commits such act on a person in detention, serving a sentence of imprisonment, protective treatment, protective custody, protective or institutional education, or in another place where personal freedom is restricted, or

c) if he causes serious harm to health by such act.

(4) By imprisonment for ten to eighteen years, the offender shall be punished if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

Sexual Coercion

(1) Whoever compels another by violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious harm to sexual self-gratification, to undressing or other comparable behavior, or whoever induces another to such behavior by abusing his defenselessness, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years or prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender who induces another to sexual intercourse, sexual self-gratification, undressing or other comparable behavior by abusing his dependence or his position and the resulting trustworthiness or influence shall be punished in the same way.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

(4) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon,

b) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a person in detention, serving a sentence of imprisonment, protective treatment, protective custody, protective or institutional education, or in another place where personal freedom is restricted, or

c) if he commits such act as a member of an organized group.

(5) By imprisonment for five to twelve years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a child under fifteen years of age, or

b) if he causes serious harm to health by such act.

(6) By imprisonment for ten to sixteen years, the offender shall be punished if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

Sexual Abuse

(1) Whoever performs coitus with a child under fifteen years of age or whoever sexually abuses such child in another manner shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to eight years.

(2) By imprisonment for two years to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a child under fifteen years of age entrusted to his supervision, abusing his dependence or his position and the resulting trustworthiness or influence.

(3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years, the offender shall be punished if he causes serious harm to health by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(4) By imprisonment for ten to eighteen years, the offender shall be punished if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

Coitus Between Relatives

Whoever performs coitus with a relative in direct line or with a sibling shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

Pimping

(1) Whoever induces, procures, recruits, entices or lures another to engage in prostitution, or whoever profits from prostitution engaged in by another, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) with intent to obtain substantial benefit for himself or another, or

b) as a member of an organized group.

(3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years or forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he causes serious harm to health by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(4) By imprisonment for eight to fifteen years or forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

Prostitution Endangering the Moral Development of Children

(1) Whoever engages in prostitution in the vicinity of a school, educational or other similar facility or place that is reserved or designated for the stay or visit of children shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever organizes, guards or otherwise ensures the engagement in prostitution in the vicinity of a school, educational or other similar facility or place that is reserved or designated for the stay or visit of children shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to five years or forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) in at least two such places, or

Dissemination of Pornography

(1) Whoever produces, imports, exports, transports, offers, makes publicly accessible, mediates, puts into circulation, sells or otherwise procures for another a photographic, film, computer, electronic or other pornographic work that manifests violence or disrespect for a human being, or that depicts, portrays or otherwise represents sexual intercourse with an animal, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) Whoever offers, leaves or makes accessible to a child a written, photographic, film, computer, electronic or other pornographic work, or

a) offers, leaves or makes accessible to a child, or

b) displays or otherwise makes accessible in a place that is accessible to children, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a member of an organized group,

b) by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means, or

c) with intent to obtain substantial benefit for himself or another.

(4) By imprisonment for one year to five years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a member of an organized group operating in multiple states, or

b) with intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another.

Production and Other Handling of Child Pornography

(1) Whoever possesses a photographic, film, computer, electronic or other pornographic work that portrays or otherwise uses a child or a person who appears to be a child shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever obtains access to child pornography by means of information or communication technology shall be punished in the same way.

(3) Whoever produces, imports, exports, transports, offers, makes publicly accessible, mediates, puts into circulation, sells or otherwise procures for another a photographic, film, computer, electronic or other pornographic work that portrays or otherwise uses a child or a person who appears to be a child, or whoever profits from such pornographic work, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(4) By imprisonment for two years to six years or forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 3

a) as a member of an organized group,

b) by print, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means, or

c) with intent to obtain substantial benefit for himself or another.

(5) By imprisonment for three years to eight years or forfeiture of property, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 3

a) as a member of an organized group operating in multiple states, or

b) with intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another.

Abuse of Child for Pornography Production

(1) Whoever induces, procures, recruits, entices, lures or abuses a child for the production of pornographic work or profits from the participation of a child in such pornographic work shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for two years to six years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organized group, or

b) with intent to obtain substantial benefit for himself or another.

(3) By imprisonment for three years to eight years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organized group operating in multiple states, or

b) with intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another.

Attendance at Pornographic Performance

Whoever attends a pornographic performance or other similar performance in which a child performs shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

Establishing Unlawful Contact with a Child

Whoever proposes a meeting to a child under fifteen years of age with intent to commit a criminal offense under § 187 para. 1, § 192, 193, § 202 para. 3 or another sexually motivated criminal offense shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST THE FAMILY AND CHILDREN

Bigamy

(1) Whoever, during the existence of his marriage, contracts another marriage shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever contracts a marriage with a person who is already in another marriage shall be punished in the same way.

Abandonment of a Child or Person Entrusted

(1) Whoever abandons a child or another person whom he is obliged to care for and who cannot obtain help on his own, and thereby exposes him to danger of death or harm to health, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) By imprisonment for one year to five years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

(3) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished if he causes serious harm to health by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(4) By imprisonment for three years to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

Neglect of Duty of Maintenance

(1) Whoever fails to fulfill, even through negligence, his legal obligation to maintain or provide for another for a period longer than four months shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) Whoever intentionally evades the fulfillment of his legal obligation to maintain or provide for another for a period longer than four months shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he places the entitled person in danger of destitution by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

Special Provisions on Punishment

To the offender of the criminal offense of neglect of duty of maintenance (§ 196), the court may impose as appropriate restrictions and appropriate obligations referred to in § 48 para. 4 also an appropriate restriction to refrain from operating motor vehicles. The court shall impose this appropriate restriction especially when there is reasonable concern that the obligation to pay owed maintenance will be obstructed or made more difficult.

Special Provisions on Effective Remorse

Criminal liability for the criminal offense of neglect of duty of maintenance (§ 196) shall cease if the criminal offense had no permanently adverse consequences and the offender subsequently fulfilled his obligation before the court of first instance began to announce the judgment.

Ill-treatment of Person Entrusted

(1) Whoever ill-treats a person who is in his care or education shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a particularly cruel or tormenting manner,

b) if he causes serious harm to health by such act,

c) if he commits such act on at least two persons, or

d) if he commits such act over a prolonged period.

(3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years, the offender shall be punished if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) serious harm to health to at least two persons, or

Ill-treatment of Person Living in Shared Dwelling

(1) Whoever ill-treats a close person or another person living with him in a shared dwelling shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years.

(2) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a particularly cruel or tormenting manner,

b) if he causes serious harm to health by such act,

c) if he commits such act on at least two persons, or

d) if he commits such act over a prolonged period.

(3) By imprisonment for five to twelve years, the offender shall be punished if he causes by the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) serious harm to health to at least two persons, or

Kidnapping of a Child and Person Afflicted with Mental Disorder

(1) Whoever removes a child or person afflicted with a mental disorder from the care of the person who has the obligation to care for them under another legal regulation or under an official decision shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or a monetary penalty.

(2) By imprisonment for one year to five years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with intent to procure property benefit for himself or another, or

b) if he endangers the moral development of the kidnapped person by such act.

(3) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he causes serious harm to health by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

c) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

(4) By imprisonment for three years to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act.

Endangering the Upbringing of a Child

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, endangers the intellectual, emotional or moral development of a child by

a) leading him to an idle or immoral life,

b) enabling him to lead an idle or immoral life,

c) enabling him to procure means for himself or another by criminal activity or other reprehensible manner, or

d) seriously violating his obligation to care for him or another important obligation arising from parental responsibility, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever enables, even through negligence, a child to play on a gaming machine equipped with technical equipment that influences the result of the game and provides the possibility of monetary winnings shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, a monetary penalty or prohibition of activities.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to five years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 from a reprehensible motive,

b) if he continues to commit such act over a prolonged period,

c) if he commits such act repeatedly, or

d) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

Solicitation for Sexual Intercourse

(1) Whoever offers, promises or provides payment, advantage or benefit to a child or another for sexual intercourse with a child, sexual self-gratification of a child, his undressing or other comparable behavior for the purpose of sexual gratification shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or a monetary penalty.

(2) Whoever performs sexual intercourse with a child or participates in his sexual self-gratification, undressing or other comparable behavior for the purpose of his own sexual gratification, if payment, advantage or benefit was offered, promised or provided to this child or another for it, shall be punished in the same way.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to five years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a child under fifteen years of age,

b) if he commits such act from a reprehensible motive,

c) if he continues to commit such act over a prolonged period, or

d) if he commits such act repeatedly.

Impunity of Child

A child who requests or accepts payment or other advantage or benefit for sexual intercourse with him, his sexual self-gratification, undressing or other comparable behavior shall not be punishable for such act, not even under provisions on instigator or accomplice.

Serving Alcohol to a Child

Whoever sells, serves or provides alcohol to a child to a greater extent or repeatedly shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST PROPERTY

Theft

(1) Whoever appropriates property of another by taking possession of it, and

a) causes not insignificant damage to the property of another thereby,

c) immediately after the act attempts to retain the thing by violence or threat of immediate violence,

d) commits the act on a thing that another has on or with him, or

e) commits the act in a territory where evacuation of persons is being carried out or has been carried out, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) Whoever appropriates property of another by taking possession of it, and was convicted or punished for such act in the last three years, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

(4) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening life or health of people, public order or property, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(5) By imprisonment for five to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, criminal offense of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f).

Embezzlement

(1) Whoever appropriates property of another that was entrusted to him, and causes not insignificant damage to the property of another thereby, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and was convicted or punished for such act in the last three years.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(4) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he commits such act as a person who has a specially imposed obligation to protect the interests of the injured party,

c) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening life or health of people, public order or property, or

d) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(5) By imprisonment for five to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, criminal offense of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f).

Unauthorized Use of Property of Another

(1) Whoever takes possession of property of another of not small value or a motor vehicle with intent to use it temporarily, or whoever causes not small damage to the property of another by temporarily using without authorization such thing that was entrusted to him, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activities.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years or prohibition of activities, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a person who has a specially imposed obligation to protect the interests of the injured party,

b) if he commits such act as a member of an organized group, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, criminal offense of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f).

Unauthorized Interference with Right to House, Apartment or Non-residential Premises
