(1) Whoever unlawfully occupies or uses a house, apartment or non-residential premises of another shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or a monetary penalty.

(2) Whoever unlawfully prevents an entitled person from using a house, apartment or non-residential premises shall be punished in the same way.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group, or

b) if he causes large-scale damage by such act.

Fraud

(1) Whoever enriches himself or another by misleading someone, exploiting someone's error or concealing material facts, and causes not insignificant damage to the property of another thereby, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and was convicted or punished for such act in the last three years.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(4) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he commits such act as a person who has a specially imposed obligation to protect the interests of the injured party,

c) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening life or health of people, public order or property, or

d) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(5) By imprisonment for five to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, criminal offense of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f).

Insurance Fraud

(1) Whoever states false or grossly distorted information or conceals material information

a) in connection with the conclusion or modification of an insurance contract,

b) in connection with the settlement of an insured event, or

c) when asserting the right to payment from insurance or other similar performance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) Whoever, with intent to procure benefit for himself or another, causes or feigns an event with which the right to payment from insurance or other similar performance is connected, or maintains a state caused by an insured event, and causes not insignificant damage to the property of another thereby, shall be punished in the same way.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and was convicted or punished for such act in the last three years.

(4) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

(5) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he commits such act as a person who has a specially imposed obligation to protect the interests of the injured party, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(6) By imprisonment for five to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, criminal offense of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f).

Credit Fraud

(1) Whoever states false or grossly distorted information or conceals material information when negotiating a credit agreement or when drawing credit shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activities.

(2) Whoever, without the consent of the creditor, uses funds obtained by purpose credit for other than the designated purpose, to a not small extent, shall be punished in the same way.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and was convicted or punished for such act in the last three years.

(4) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

(5) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he commits such act as a person who has a specially imposed obligation to protect the interests of the injured party, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(6) By imprisonment for five to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, criminal offense of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f).

Subsidy Fraud

(1) Whoever states false or grossly distorted information or conceals material information in an application for the provision of a grant, subsidy or refundable financial assistance or contribution shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activities.

(2) Whoever uses funds obtained by purpose grant, subsidy or refundable financial assistance or contribution for other than the designated purpose, to a not small extent, shall be punished in the same way.

(3) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and was convicted or punished for such act in the last three years.

(4) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

(5) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he commits such act as a person who has a specially imposed obligation to protect the interests of the injured party, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(6) By imprisonment for five to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he commits such act with intent to enable or facilitate the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, criminal offense of financing terrorism (§ 312d) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f).

Operating Dishonest Games and Bets

(1) Whoever operates a monetary or other similar game or bet whose rules do not guarantee equal chances of winning to all participants shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or prohibition of activities.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and was convicted or punished for such act in the last three years.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years or a monetary penalty, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he obtains greater benefit for himself or another by such act.

(4) By imprisonment for two years to eight years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he causes substantial damage by such act, or

c) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

(5) By imprisonment for five to ten years, the offender shall be punished,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act.

## § 214, § 215 repealed

Money Laundering

(1) Whoever conceals, transfers to himself or herself or to another, harbours or uses a thing which is proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad by another person, or whoever converts such a thing with the intent to enable another person to escape criminal prosecution, punishment or protective measure or their execution, or whoever conspires to commit such a criminal offence, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years, by a fine, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever disguises the origin of a thing which is proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad, in particular by concealing or hiding its true nature, location, movement, disposition thereof, ownership or other right thereto, or whoever otherwise endeavours to substantially hinder or prevent the determination of its origin, or whoever conspires to commit such a criminal offence, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years or by a fine,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in relation to a thing originating from a felony,

b) if he or she commits such an act in relation to a thing which has greater value, or

c) if he or she obtains greater benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he or she commits such an act in relation to a thing originating from a particularly serious felony,

c) if he or she commits such an act in relation to a thing which has substantial value,

d) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act, or

e) if he or she commits such an act because he or she violated an important duty arising from his or her employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him or her pursuant to law. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in connection with an organised group operating in multiple states,

b) if he or she commits such an act in relation to a thing which has value on a large scale, or

c) if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by such an act. Special Provision on Punishment

In determining the type of punishment and its extent for the criminal offence of money laundering pursuant to § 216(1) and (2), the court shall also take into account the level of the sentence established for the criminal offence from which the proceeds from criminal activity originate, if the criminal law establishes a more lenient punishment for such criminal offence. Money Laundering by Negligence

(1) Whoever by negligence enables another to disguise the origin or determination of the origin of a thing of greater value which is proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall apply to whoever by negligence conceals, transfers to himself or herself or to another, harbours or uses a thing of greater value which is proceeds from criminal activity committed in the territory of the Czech Republic or abroad by another person. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 because he or she violated an important duty arising from his or her employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him or her pursuant to law,

b) if he or she commits such an act in relation to a thing originating from a felony,

c) if he or she commits such an act in relation to a thing which has substantial value, or

d) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in relation to a thing originating from a particularly serious felony,

b) if he or she commits such an act in relation to a thing which has value on a large scale, or

c) if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by such an act. Common Provision

For the purposes of § 216 and 217, criminal activity committed abroad means an act committed abroad which fulfils the elements of a criminal offence under the law of the Czech Republic, regardless of whether it is also punishable under the law of the state in whose territory it was committed. Usury

(1) Whoever, by abusing someone's mental weakness, distress, inexperience, recklessness or someone's agitation, causes himself or herself or another to be provided with or promised a performance whose value is in gross disproportion to the value of the reciprocal performance, or whoever asserts such a claim or transfers it to himself or herself with the intent to assert it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,

a) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) if he or she commits such an act as a member of an organised group, or

c) if he or she causes another person to be in a state of severe need by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or a state of war, during a natural disaster or other event seriously threatening the life or health of people, public order or property, or

b) if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by such an act. Concealment of a Thing

(1) Whoever appropriates another's thing of not insignificant value which has come into his or her possession by finding, by mistake or otherwise without the consent of the person entitled, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Breach of Duty in Administration of Another's Property

(1) Whoever violates a duty imposed on him or her by law or assumed by contract to safeguard or administer another's property, and thereby causes not insignificant damage to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the interests of the injured party, or

b) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Breach of Duty in Administration of Another's Property by Negligence

(1) Whoever by gross negligence violates an important duty imposed on him or her by law or assumed by contract in safeguarding or administering another's property, and thereby causes substantial damage to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the interests of the injured party, or

b) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by such an act. Damaging a Creditor

(1) Whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of his or her creditor by

a) destroying, damaging, concealing, alienating, rendering unusable, or removing even only a part of his or her property,

b) assigning his or her claim, or assuming another's debt,

c) encumbering a thing which is the subject of an obligation, or leasing it,

d) simulating or acknowledging a non-existent right or obligation,

e) simulating or acknowledging a right or obligation to a greater extent than corresponds to reality,

f) simulating the fulfilment of an obligation, or

g) simulating insolvency or otherwise ostensibly reducing his or her property or simulating its extinction, and thereby causes not insignificant damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall apply to whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of another person's creditor by

a) destroying, damaging, concealing, alienating, rendering unusable or removing even only a part of the debtor's property, or

b) asserting to the debtor's property a non-existent right or claim or an existing right or claim in a higher value or better ranking than it has, and thereby causes not insignificant damage to another's property. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years,

a) if he or she causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2,

b) if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by such an act, or

c) if he or she causes another's insolvency by such an act. Preference of a Creditor

(1) Whoever as a debtor who is insolvent frustrates, even if only partially, the satisfaction of his or her creditor by giving preference to another creditor, and thereby causes not insignificant damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he or she causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he or she causes another's insolvency by such an act. Causing Insolvency

(1) Whoever, even by gross negligence, causes his or her own insolvency by

a) making expenditures grossly disproportionate to his or her property situation,

b) administering his or her property in a manner which does not correspond to the duties imposed on him or her by law or assumed by contract or is in gross disproportion to them,

c) using a provided credit contrary to or in gross disproportion to its purpose,

d) providing loans or credits from his or her property to other persons, although this is in gross disproportion to his or her property situation, or

e) making, beyond the scope of ordinary business risk, a transaction or operation which does not belong to his or her regular business activity or is in gross disproportion to his or her property situation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall apply to whoever, even by gross negligence, accepts a new obligation or establishes a pledge, although he or she knows that he or she is insolvent, and thereby worsens the position of existing creditors. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years if he or she causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Breach of Duty in Insolvency Proceedings

Whoever in insolvency proceedings frustrates or grossly impedes the exercise of the function of the insolvency administrator, and thereby jeopardises the purpose of insolvency proceedings, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity. Machinations in Insolvency Proceedings

(1) Whoever as a creditor in connection with creditors' voting in insolvency proceedings accepts or causes to be promised to himself or herself property or other benefit contrary to the principles and rules of insolvency proceedings, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall apply to whoever provides, offers or promises to a creditor in connection with creditors' voting in insolvency proceedings property or other benefit contrary to the principles and rules of insolvency proceedings. (3) Whoever as an insolvency administrator, member of a creditors' committee or creditors' representative in insolvency proceedings accepts or causes to be promised to himself or herself or to another to the detriment of creditors property or other benefit which he or she is not entitled to, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years,

a) if he or she causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3,

b) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act, or

c) if he or she commits such an act as an official. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years,

a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3, or

b) if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by such an act. Breach of Duty to Make a Truthful Statement of Property

Whoever in proceedings before a court or other public authority refuses to fulfil the legal duty to make a statement of property or evades such duty or in such a statement states false or grossly distorted data, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, by a fine or by prohibition of activity. Damage to Another's Thing

(1) Whoever destroys, damages or renders unusable another's thing, and thereby causes not insignificant damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall apply to whoever damages another's thing by spraying, painting or writing on it with paint or another substance. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a thing of a witness, expert or interpreter for the performance of their duty,

b) if he or she commits such an act on another's thing because of his or her actual or presumed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he or she is actually or presumably without religion,

c) if he or she commits such an act on a thing which enjoys protection under another legal regulation, or

d) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Abuse of Ownership

Whoever damages an important interest of culture, science, nature protection, landscape or environmental protection, protected by another legal regulation, by destroying, damaging, rendering unusable or squandering his or her own thing of greater value which enjoys protection under another legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. Unauthorised Access to a Computer System and Unauthorised Interference with a Computer System or Data Carrier

(1) Whoever overcomes security measures and thereby gains unauthorised access to a computer system or to a part thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever interferes with a computer system or data carrier by

a) unauthorised use of data stored in a computer system or on a data carrier,

b) unauthorised deletion or otherwise destroying, damaging, altering, suppressing, reducing the quality of or rendering unusable data stored in a computer system or on a data carrier,

c) forging or altering data stored in a computer system or on a data carrier so that they are considered genuine or acted upon as if they were genuine data, regardless of whether these data are directly readable and comprehensible, or

d) unauthorised insertion or transfer of data into a computer system or onto a data carrier or making another interference with the software or technical equipment of a computer system or other technical device for data processing, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) with the intent to cause another damage or other harm or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or herself or another, or

b) with the intent to unauthorisedly limit the functionality of a computer system or other technical device for data processing. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act,

c) if he or she commits such an act against a computer system whose disruption would have a serious impact on the functioning of the state, health of persons, security, economy or provision of basic life needs of the population,

d) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act, or

e) if he or she causes serious disruption in the activity of a legal person or natural person who is an entrepreneur by such an act. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by such an act. Procuring and Harbouring Access Devices and Passwords to a Computer System and Other Such Data

(1) Whoever manufactures, puts into circulation, imports, exports, transports, offers, mediates, sells or otherwise makes available, procures for himself or herself or for another or harbours

a) a device or its component, procedure, tool or any other means, including a computer program, created or adapted for unauthorised access to an electronic communications network, to a computer system or to a part thereof or for unauthorised interference with a computer system or data carrier, or

b) a computer password, access code, data, procedure or any other similar means by which access to a computer system or part thereof can be gained, with the intent that it be used to commit the criminal offence of breach of secrecy of transmitted messages pursuant to § 182(1)(b) or (c) or the criminal offence of unauthorised access to a computer system and unauthorised interference with a computer system or data carrier pursuant to § 230(1) or (2), shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by forfeiture of a thing or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group, or

b) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Unauthorised Interference with a Computer System or Data Carrier by Negligence

(1) Whoever by gross negligence in breach of a duty arising from employment, profession, position or function or imposed pursuant to law or assumed by contract

a) destroys, damages, alters or renders unusable data stored in a computer system or on a data carrier, or

b) makes an interference with the technical or software equipment of a computer system or other technical device for data processing, and thereby causes substantial damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1. ECONOMIC CRIMES

Criminal Offences against Currency and Payment Instruments

Counterfeiting and Alteration of Money

(1) Whoever procures for himself or herself or for another or harbours counterfeit or altered money or elements of money serving to protect against their counterfeiting, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (2) Whoever counterfeits or alters money with the intent to pass it off as genuine or valid or as money of higher value, or whoever passes off counterfeit or altered money as genuine or valid or as money of higher value, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group, or

b) if he or she commits such an act on a substantial scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group operating in multiple states, or

b) if he or she commits such an act on a large scale. Unauthorised Procuring, Counterfeiting and Alteration of a Payment Instrument

(1) Whoever procures for himself or herself or for another, makes available, accepts or harbours without the consent of the authorised user a payment instrument which enables cash withdrawal or transfer of funds or virtual assets used instead of funds (hereinafter "payment instrument") and which belongs to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) Whoever procures for himself or herself or for another, makes available, accepts or harbours a counterfeit or altered payment instrument, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (3) Whoever counterfeits or alters a payment instrument with the intent to use it as genuine or valid, or whoever uses a counterfeit or altered payment instrument as genuine or valid, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 as a member of an organised group, or

b) if he or she commits such an act on a substantial scale. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 as a member of an organised group operating in multiple states, or

b) if he or she commits such an act on a large scale. Passing Counterfeit and Altered Money

Whoever passes off counterfeit or altered money with which he or she was paid as genuine, as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. Manufacture and Possession of Counterfeiting Instruments and Equipment Intended for Unauthorised Obtaining of a Payment Instrument

(1) Whoever manufactures, puts into circulation, imports, exports, transports, offers, sells, mediates or otherwise makes available, procures for himself or herself or for another or harbours an instrument, device or its component, procedure, aid or any other means, including a computer program, created or adapted for counterfeiting or alteration of money or elements serving to protect money against counterfeiting or created or adapted for counterfeiting, alteration or unauthorised obtaining of a payment instrument, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in the exercise of his or her profession. Unauthorised Manufacture of Money

(1) Whoever without authorisation, using equipment or materials intended and held for the manufacture of money in accordance with law, manufactures money or elements serving to protect money against counterfeiting, or whoever procures for himself or herself or for another, puts into circulation or harbours money or elements serving to protect money against counterfeiting manufactured without authorisation, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group, or

b) if he or she commits such an act on a substantial scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group operating in multiple states, or

b) if he or she commits such an act on a large scale. Common Provision

Protection pursuant to § 233 to 237 is also provided to money and payment instruments other than domestic and to domestic and foreign securities. Endangering Circulation of Domestic Money

(1) Whoever without authorisation manufactures or issues substitutes for domestic money, or whoever puts such substitutes into circulation without authorisation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The same punishment shall apply to whoever

a) refuses domestic money without lawful grounds, or

Tax, Fee and Foreign Exchange Criminal Offences

Evasion of Tax, Fee and Similar Mandatory Payment

(1) Whoever on a greater scale evades tax, customs duty, social security contribution, contribution for state employment policy, accident insurance contribution, health insurance contribution, fee or other similar mandatory payment or obtains an advantage on any of these mandatory payments, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 together with at least two persons,

b) if he or she breaks an official seal to facilitate such an act, or

c) if he or she commits such an act on a substantial scale. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale, or

b) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 2(c) in connection with an organised group operating in multiple states. Failure to Remit Tax, Social Security Contribution and Similar Mandatory Payment

(1) Whoever on a greater scale fails to fulfil his or her legal duty as an employer or payer to remit for an employee or another person tax, social security contribution, contribution for state employment policy or health insurance contribution, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Special Provision on Effective Regret

Criminal liability for the criminal offence of failure to remit tax, social security contribution and similar mandatory payment (§ 241) ceases to exist if the offender subsequently fulfilled his or her duty before the first instance court began to pronounce the judgment. Failure to Fulfil Notification Duty in Tax Proceedings

(1) Whoever fails to fulfil his or her legal notification duty towards the tax administrator, and thereby jeopardises on a greater scale the proper and timely determination of tax for another or its collection from another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to four years or by a fine if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale. Breach of Regulations on Labels and Other Items for Marking Goods

(1) Whoever handles labels, control strips or other items for marking goods for tax purposes contrary to another legal regulation with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or herself or for another, or whoever contrary to another legal regulation imports, stores, transports or puts into circulation goods without labels, control strips or other items for their marking for tax purposes, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 together with at least two persons, or

b) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Counterfeiting and Alteration of Items for Marking Goods for Tax Purposes and Items Evidencing Fulfilment of Fee Obligation

(1) Whoever counterfeits or alters labels, control strips or other items for marking goods for tax purposes or items issued by a public authority or a legal person authorised by it as evidence of fulfilment of a fee obligation with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or herself or for another, or whoever puts such labels, strips or items into circulation or uses them as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale, or

b) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Counterfeiting and Alteration of Stamps

(1) Whoever counterfeits or alters postal or revenue stamps with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised benefit for himself or herself or for another, or whoever intentionally puts such stamps into circulation or uses them as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale, or

b) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by the act referred to in paragraph 1. Endangering Foreign Exchange Management

Whoever during a state of emergency breaches a prohibition imposed by a measure issued on the basis of the crisis act in relation to foreign exchange management and thereby causes serious jeopardy to payment ability towards abroad or internal monetary balance of the Czech Republic, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years, by a fine or by prohibition of activity. Criminal Offences against Mandatory Rules of Market Economy and Circulation of Goods in Relations with Abroad

Breach of Regulations on Rules of Economic Competition

(1) Whoever breaches another legal regulation on unfair competition by engaging in economic competition in

b) misleading designation of goods and services,

c) causing danger of confusion,

d) parasitising on the reputation of an enterprise, products or services of another competitor,

h) breach of trade secrets, or

i) endangering consumers' health and the environment, and thereby causes harm on a greater scale to other competitors or consumers or thereby obtains unauthorised advantages on a greater scale for himself or herself or for another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of a thing. whoever contrary to another legal regulation on protection of economic competition concludes with his or her competitor an agreement on price determination, an agreement on market division or another agreement distorting economic competition, whoever contrary to another legal regulation on public procurement breaches in a serious manner mandatory rules of tender proceedings, or whoever contrary to another legal regulation governing the activity of persons authorised to do business in the financial market, the activity of banks and other legal persons authorised to carry out financial activity, trading in investment instruments, pension insurance and insurance business, management and administration of investment funds or foreign investment funds, breaches in a serious manner mandatory rules of prudent business conduct, property management, professional care or the prohibition to perform tasks, services or other activities determined by law or official decision, and thereby causes harm on a greater scale to other competitors or consumers, to the contracting authority or to another supplier or thereby obtains unauthorised advantages on a greater scale for himself or herself or for another. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,

a) if he or she commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he or she commits such an act repeatedly,

c) if he or she causes substantial damage by such an act, or

d) if he or she obtains substantial benefit for himself or herself or for another by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he or she causes damage on a large scale by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2,

b) if he or she obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or herself or for another by such an act, or

c) if he or she causes another's insolvency by such an act. Special Provision on Effective Regret

Criminal liability for the criminal offence of breach of regulations on rules of economic competition pursuant to § 248(2) first subparagraph ceases to exist if the offender fulfils the conditions pursuant to another legal regulation on protection of economic competition for refraining from imposing a fine or for reduction of a fine for participation in an agreement on price determination, an agreement on market division or another agreement distorting economic competition with his or her competitor. Unauthorised Issue of a Security

(1) Whoever with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain unauthorised advantage or benefit for himself or herself or for another puts into circulation as a security a document, although the conditions established by law for its issue are not fulfilled, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall apply to whoever with the intent referred to in paragraph 1 has performed a registration of the issue of a book-entry security in the legally established register of securities, although the conditions established by law for such registration are not fulfilled.# Manipulation of Investment Instrument Exchange Rates

(1) Whoever, with the intent to influence the price or exchange rate of investment instruments that are admitted to trading in a trading system or for which admission to trading in a trading system has been applied for,

a) disseminates or provides to another false or grossly distorted information significantly affecting the price or exchange rate of such investment instruments or commodities related to them traded on an organised commodity market, or the calculation of rates, indices or quantitatively expressed indicators determining the price or exchange rate of such investment instruments, or

b) carries out a transaction, gives an order or engages in other conduct capable of giving a false impression of the supply, demand, price or exchange rate of such investment instrument or commodities related to it traded on an organised commodity market, or of the value of a rate, index or quantitatively expressed indicator determining the price or exchange rate of such investment instruments,

shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he obtains substantial benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he obtains large-scale benefit by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

# Unauthorised Business Activity

(1) Whoever, without authorisation, provides services or carries on production, trade or other business activity on a larger scale shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,

a) if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act.

# Unauthorised Operation of Gambling

(1) Whoever, without authorisation, operates, organises, promotes or intermediates gambling shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group, or

b) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

# Harming the Consumer

(1) Whoever causes not insignificant damage to another's property by harming consumers, in particular by deceiving them as to the quality, quantity or weight of goods, or whoever places on the market on a larger scale products, works or services and thereby conceals their essential defects, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years or by a fine,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act, or

c) if he has been convicted of such act within the last five years or released from serving a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

# Distortion of Data on the State of Economic Activity and Assets

(1) Whoever fails to maintain account books, records or other documents serving for an overview of the state of economic activity and property or for their control, although he is obliged to do so by law, whoever enters false or grossly distorted data in such account books, records or other documents, or whoever alters, destroys, damages, renders unusable or conceals such account books, records or other documents, and thereby endangers the property rights of another or the timely and proper assessment of tax, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever enters false or grossly distorted data in documents serving for entry into the commercial register, foundation register, register of public benefit corporations or register of communities of unit owners, or conceals essential facts in such documents, whoever enters false or grossly distorted data in documents serving for the preparation of an expert opinion attached to an application for entry into the commercial register, foundation register, register of public benefit corporations or register of communities of unit owners, or conceals essential data in such documents, or whoever endangers or restricts another in his rights by failing without undue delay to file an application for entry of data required by law into the commercial register, foundation register, register of public benefit corporations or register of communities of unit owners, or to deposit a document in the collection of documents, although he is obliged to do so by law or contract.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he causes substantial damage to another's property by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he causes large-scale damage to another's property by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

# Misuse of Information in Trade

(1) Whoever, without authorisation, publishes, makes accessible or communicates to a third person information not yet publicly accessible, the publication of which is capable of substantially influencing decision-making in trade, and thereby causes greater damage or other serious consequence, or thereby secures greater benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) Whoever, without authorisation, uses information not yet publicly accessible, which he obtained in the performance of his employment, profession, position, function or otherwise and the publication of which is capable of substantially influencing decision-making in trade, by carrying out or giving cause to carry out a contract or operation in a trading system or on an organised commodity market, and thereby causes greater damage or other serious consequence, or thereby secures greater benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years or by prohibition of activities.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he causes substantial damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act.

# Abuse of Position in Trade

(1) Whoever, as an entrepreneur, partner, member of a body, employee or participant in the business of two or more entrepreneurs with the same or similar object of activity, with the intent to secure an advantage or benefit for himself or another, concludes or gives cause to conclude a contract to the detriment of one or more entrepreneurs or their enterprises, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

# Securing an Advantage in the Award of Public Contracts, in Public Competitions and Public Auctions

(1) Whoever, in connection with the award of a public contract, a public competition or a public auction, with the intent to cause damage to another or to secure benefit for himself or another, secures for a tenderer, competitor or auction participant an advantage or more favourable conditions to the detriment of other tenderers or competitors, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an evaluation committee, organiser or convener of a public competition or public auction, auctioneer or as a member of an organised group,

b) he causes substantial damage by such act, or

c) he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

(3) The same punishment as in paragraph 2 shall be imposed on whoever, under the circumstances referred to in paragraph 1, requests, accepts or has promised to himself proprietary or other benefit.

(4) Whoever, under the circumstances referred to in paragraph 1, requests, accepts or has promised to himself proprietary or other benefit and commits such act as an official person shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years.

# Collusion in the Award of Public Contracts and in Public Competitions

(1) Whoever engages in collusion in connection with the award of a public contract or a public competition by

a) inducing another by deceit or threat of violence or other serious harm to refrain from participating in the award procedure or in a public competition,

b) providing, offering or promising to another proprietary or other benefit in exchange for refraining from participating in the award procedure or in a public competition,

c) requesting or accepting proprietary or other benefit in exchange for refraining from participating in the award procedure or in a public competition, or

d) engaging, on the basis of an agreement with another interested party or tenderer, in activities aimed at the award of a public contract at a disproportionately high or otherwise disadvantageous price,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) with the intent to secure substantial benefit for himself or another, or

b) as a member of an organised group.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) with the intent to secure large-scale benefit for himself or another, or

b) as an official person with the intent to secure substantial benefit for himself or another.

# Collusion in Public Auctions

(1) Whoever engages in collusion in a public auction of a thing by

a) inducing another by deceit or threat of violence or other serious harm to refrain from participating in the submission of bids at the auction,

b) providing, offering or promising to another proprietary or other benefit in exchange for refraining from submitting bids at the auction, or

c) requesting or accepting proprietary or other benefit in exchange for refraining from submitting bids at the auction,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) with the intent to secure substantial benefit for himself or another, or

b) as a member of an organised group.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) with the intent to secure large-scale benefit for himself or another, or

b) as an official person with the intent to secure substantial benefit for himself or another.

# Issuance of False Certificate and Report

Whoever, in the name of a bank or other entrepreneur authorised to carry on financial activities under another legal regulation, issues to another a false certificate concerning his financial situation or his property circumstances, or whoever, as an auditor, issues to another a false auditor's report or a false certificate concerning the financial situation or property circumstances, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activities.

# Damage to the Financial Interests of the European Union

(1) Whoever prepares, uses or submits false, incorrect or incomplete documents, provides false, incorrect or incomplete data, or conceals documents or data, and thereby enables the unauthorised use or retention of financial resources originating from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, or the reduction of resources of any such budget, or enables the unauthorised use or retention of assets acquired from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, without authorisation, uses financial resources originating from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, assets acquired from the budget of the European Union or budgets managed by the European Union or on its behalf, or reduces the resources of any such budget.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he causes greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he commits such act as a person having a specially imposed duty to protect the interests of the European Union, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2.

# Breach of Regulations on the Movement of Goods in Relations with Foreign Countries

(1) Whoever substantially endangers the public interest by breaching a prohibition, restriction or other important obligation established for the import, export or transit of goods shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with at least two persons,

b) if he causes substantial damage by such act, or

c) if he causes or increases by such act the danger of the introduction or spread of an infectious disease of animals in interest breeding, farm animals or free-living animals, or an infectious disease or pest of useful plants.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he causes or increases by such act the danger of the introduction or spread of an infectious human disease.

# Breach of Regulations on Export Control of Dual-Use Goods and Technologies

Whoever exports dual-use goods or technologies without a valid authorisation shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years, by a fine or by forfeiture of property.

# Breach of Obligations in the Export of Dual-Use Goods and Technologies

(1) Whoever breaches or fails to fulfil an important obligation arising from his employment, profession, position or function, and thereby causes an authorisation for the export of dual-use goods or technologies to be issued without authorisation, or causes such goods to escape from records, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine,

a) if, as a result of the act referred to in paragraph 1, the goods were exported,

b) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for himself or another, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group operating in several states,

b) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another, or

c) if he causes large-scale damage by such act.

# Distortion of Data and Failure to Maintain Records Regarding the Export of Dual-Use Goods and Technologies

(1) Whoever obtains, on the basis of false or incomplete data, the issuance of an authorisation for the export of dual-use goods or technologies shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever destroys, damages, renders unusable, conceals the documents necessary for the recording of the export of dual-use goods or technologies, or fails to maintain records, or whoever interferes with the technical or software equipment of a computer system in which records of such goods and technologies are maintained.

# Conduct of Foreign Trade in Military Material without Authorisation or Licence

(1) Whoever conducts foreign trade in military material without authorisation or licence shall be punished by imprisonment for one to eight years, by a fine or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group,

b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war,

c) if he commits such act repeatedly,

d) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act,

e) if he causes large-scale damage by such act,

f) if he commits such act in relation to significant military material, or

g) if he causes by such act that military material reaches an area of war or other armed conflict or a state where there is an increased danger of war or other armed conflict.

# Breach of Obligation in Connection with the Issuance of Authorisation and Licence for Foreign Trade in Military Material

(1) Whoever breaches or fails to fulfil an important obligation arising from his employment, profession, position or function, and thereby causes an authorisation to conduct foreign trade in military material or a licence for a specific trade in military material to be issued without authorisation, or issues a false or incomplete document on the basis of which such authorisation or licence is issued without authorisation, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years or by a fine,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group,

b) if he causes by such act that military material has been taken abroad,

c) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain substantial benefit for himself or another,

d) if he causes substantial damage by such act, or

e) if he commits such act in relation to significant military material.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in connection with an organised group operating in several states,

b) if he commits such act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another,

c) if he causes large-scale damage by such act, or

d) if he causes by such act that military material reaches an area of war or other armed conflict or a state where there is an increased danger of war or other armed conflict.

# Distortion of Data and Failure to Maintain Records Regarding Foreign Trade in Military Material

(1) Whoever supports an application for authorisation or for a licence for foreign trade in military material with a false or incomplete document, or conceals facts important for the issuance of the authorisation or licence, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever destroys, damages, renders unusable, conceals the documents necessary for the recording of foreign trade in military material, or fails to maintain records, or whoever interferes with the technical or software equipment of a computer system in which records of foreign trade in military material are maintained.

# Criminal Offences against Industrial Rights and Copyright

## Infringement of Rights to a Trademark and Other Designations

(1) Whoever places into circulation products or provides services unlawfully marked with a trademark to which another has exclusive rights, or with a mark confusingly similar thereto, or offers, intermediates, manufactures, imports, exports or otherwise procures or keeps such products for this purpose, or offers or intermediates such service for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever, for the purpose of achieving economic benefit, unlawfully uses a business name or any designation confusingly similar thereto, or places into circulation products or services unlawfully bearing a designation of origin or a geographical indication or a designation confusingly similar thereto, or offers, intermediates, manufactures, imports, exports or otherwise procures or keeps such products or services for this purpose for himself or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,

a) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he commits such act on a substantial scale.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) if he commits such act on a large scale.

## Infringement of Protected Industrial Rights

(1) Whoever unlawfully interferes in a not insignificant manner with the rights to a protected invention, industrial design, utility model or topography of a semiconductor product shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,

a) if the act referred to in paragraph 1 shows signs of commercial activity or other business,

b) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act, or

c) if he commits such act on a substantial scale.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act on a large scale.

## Infringement of Copyright, Rights Related to Copyright and Rights to a Database

(1) Whoever unlawfully interferes in a not insignificant manner with the rights protected by law to a copyright work, artistic performance, sound or audiovisual recording, radio or television broadcast, press publication or database shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of a thing,

a) if the act referred to in paragraph 1 shows signs of commercial activity or other business,

b) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another, or causes substantial damage to another, by such act, or

c) if he commits such act on a substantial scale.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years,

a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another, or causes large-scale damage to another, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act on a large scale.

## Forgery and Imitation of a Work of Fine Art

(1) Whoever forges a copyright work of fine art or imitates the artistic expression of another author with the intent that the new work be regarded as an original work of such author shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act, or

c) if he commits such act on a substantial scale.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years,

a) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act on a large scale.

# GENERALLY DANGEROUS CRIMINAL OFFENCES

## Generally Endangering Criminal Offences

### General Endangerment

(1) Whoever intentionally causes general danger by exposing people to danger of death or serious bodily harm, or another's property to danger of large-scale damage, by causing fire or flood or the harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces, or by committing another similarly dangerous act, or whoever increases such general danger or makes its aversion or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he commits such act repeatedly within a short time,

c) if he causes large-scale damage by such act, or

d) if he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death by such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence,

a) if he intentionally causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

### General Endangerment by Negligence

(1) Whoever negligently causes general danger by exposing people to danger of death or serious bodily harm, or another's property to danger of large-scale damage, by causing fire or flood or the harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces, or by committing another similarly dangerous act, or whoever negligently increases such general danger or makes its aversion or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by prohibition of activities,

a) if he causes serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) if he commits such act by breaching an important obligation arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or by a fine,

a) if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he causes large-scale damage or serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 2(b).

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 2(b).

### Endangerment under the Influence of an Addictive Substance

(1) Whoever performs, in a state of incapacity which he brought upon himself by the influence of an addictive substance, employment or other activity during which he could endanger the life or health of people or cause substantial damage to property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by a fine or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, by a fine or by prohibition of activities,

a) if he causes an accident, a traffic or other accident, bodily harm to another or greater damage to another's property or other serious consequence by the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) if he commits such act in the performance of employment or other activity during which the influence of an addictive substance is particularly dangerous, especially if he operates a mass transport vehicle, or

c) if he has been convicted of such act within the last two years or released from serving a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such act.

### Breach of Obligation in Imminent Peril

Whoever frustrates or makes more difficult the aversion or mitigation of imminent peril that directly affects a larger group of persons by, without serious reason,

a) refusing assistance which is imposed on him by law or to which he has bound himself, or

b) frustrating the provision of such assistance by another person,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activities.

### Damage to and Endangerment of the Operation of a Facility Serving the Public Interest

(1) Whoever intentionally damages a facility serving the public interest or endangers its operation or use shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years or by a fine,

a) if he destroys, removes or renders unusable a facility serving the public interest,

b) if he causes disruption of the operation of a facility serving the public interest by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

c) if he causes substantial damage by such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2(a), or

b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

### Damage to and Endangerment of the Operation of a Facility Serving the Public Interest by Negligence

(1) Whoever, by gross negligence, destroys, damages, removes or renders unusable a facility serving the public interest, or endangers the operation of a facility serving the public interest, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years,

a) if he causes large-scale damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

### Damage to a Geodetic Point

(1) Whoever intentionally destroys, damages or renders unusable a mark of

a) a geodetic point of the basic geodynamic network,

b) a geodetic point of the reference network of zero order,

c) a geodetic point of the supplementary reference network of zero order,

d) a basic levelling geodetic point, or

e) an absolute gravity geodetic point,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by a fine.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever intentionally destroys, damages or renders unusable the equipment of a permanent station for receiving signals of global navigation systems of the Czech Network of Permanent Stations for Position Determination.

### Unauthorised Arming

(1) Whoever, without authorisation, manufactures, procures for himself or another, or keeps a firearm or its main parts or components, or ammunition in a larger quantity, or a prohibited weapon accessory, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activities or forfeiture of a thing.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever brings into firing condition a deactivated weapon or carries out constructional modifications to it aimed at bringing it into firing condition, or carries out constructional modifications to a weapon aimed at increasing its effectiveness, or whoever forges, alters, obliterates or removes the unique marking of a firearm that enables its identification.

(3) Whoever

a) manufactures, procures for himself or another, or keeps an explosive in a quantity greater than small, a weapon of mass destruction or components indispensably necessary for the use of such weapon, or

b) accumulates, manufactures or procures for himself or another weapons or ammunition in a substantial quantity,

shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 3 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he commits such act on a larger scale, or

c) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

### Development, Production and Possession of Prohibited Combat Means

(1) Whoever manufactures, imports, exports, transports, keeps or accumulates weapons, combat means or explosives prohibited by law or international treaty, or otherwise handles them, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or by forfeiture of property.

(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever designs, constructs or uses facilities intended for the development, production or storage of the weapons, combat means or explosives referred to in paragraph 1.
