(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organised group,

b) if he commits such act on a larger scale, or

c) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

### Unauthorised Production and Possession of Radioactive Material and Highly Dangerous Substance

(1) Whoever, without authorisation, even by gross negligence, manufactures, imports, exports, transports, keeps or procures for another radioactive material or a highly dangerous substance or items intended for its production shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years, by a fine or by prohibition of activities.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he causes serious bodily harm by the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) if he commits such act on a larger scale, or

c) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) if he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such act,

c) if he commits such act as a member of an organised group, or

d) if he commits such act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

### Highly Dangerous Substance

For the purposes of this Act, a highly dangerous substance means a substance classified in Schedule 1 in the Annex to the Convention on the Prohibition of the Development, Production, Stockpiling and Use of Chemical Weapons and on their Destruction.

### Unauthorised Production and Possession of Nuclear Material and Special Fissile Material

(1) Whoever, without authorisation, even by gross negligence, manufactures, imports, exports, transports, keeps or procures for another source nuclear material or items intended for its production shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years.(2) Whoever, without permission, even through gross negligence, manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, stores or procures for another special fissile material or items intended for its manufacture, shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years. Unauthorized manufacture and other handling of narcotic and psychotropic substances and poisons

(1) Whoever unlawfully manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, offers, brokers, sells or otherwise procures for another or stores for another a narcotic or psychotropic substance, a preparation containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance, a precursor or poison, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years or by forfeiture of property if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organized group,

b) having been convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years,

d) on a larger scale against a child or in a quantity greater than small against a child under fifteen years of age. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years or by forfeiture of property if

a) he causes grievous bodily harm by the act specified in paragraph 1,

b) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another,

c) he commits such an act on a large scale, or

d) he commits such an act on a larger scale against a child under fifteen years of age. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to eighteen years or by forfeiture of property if

a) he causes grievous bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1,

b) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another, or

c) he commits such an act in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states. Possession of narcotic and psychotropic substances and poison

(1) Whoever unlawfully possesses for his own use in a quantity greater than small a narcotic substance of cannabis, cannabis resin or a psychotropic substance containing any tetrahydrocannabinol, isomer or stereochemical variant thereof (THC), shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of thing. (2) Whoever unlawfully possesses for his own use a narcotic or psychotropic substance other than that specified in paragraph 1 or poison in a quantity greater than small, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years or by a fine if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 on a larger scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 on a considerable scale. Unauthorized cultivation of plants containing narcotic or psychotropic substances

(1) Whoever unlawfully cultivates for his own use in a quantity greater than small a cannabis plant, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by a fine or by forfeiture of thing. (2) Whoever unlawfully cultivates for his own use in a quantity greater than small a mushroom or plant other than that specified in paragraph 1 containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by a fine or by forfeiture of thing. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by a fine if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 on a larger scale. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 on a considerable scale. Manufacture and possession of items for unauthorized manufacture of narcotic and psychotropic substances and poison

(1) Whoever manufactures, procures for himself or another or stores a precursor or other item intended for the unauthorized manufacture of a narcotic or psychotropic substance, a preparation containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance, or poison, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years, by a fine, prohibition of activity or forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) he commits such an act on a considerable scale,

c) he commits such an act on a larger scale against a child, or

d) he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such an act. Spreading drug addiction

(1) Whoever induces another to abuse an addictive substance other than alcohol or supports him in it or otherwise encourages or spreads the abuse of such a substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organized group,

c) through the press, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 against a child under fifteen years of age. Manufacture and other handling of substances with hormonal effects

(1) Whoever unlawfully in a larger quantity manufactures, stores, imports, exports, transports through, offers, sells, provides or administers to another a substance with anabolic or other hormonal effects for purposes other than therapeutic, or whoever uses against another a method consisting of increasing oxygen transfer in the human body or another method with doping effects for purposes other than therapeutic, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) he commits such an act against a child under fifteen years of age, or

c) he commits such an act on a considerable scale against a child over fifteen years of age. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if

a) he causes grievous bodily harm by the act specified in paragraph 1,

b) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another,

c) he commits such an act on a considerable scale against a child under fifteen years of age,

d) he commits such an act as a physician or other qualified health professional. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if

a) he causes grievous bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1,

b) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another, or

c) he commits such an act in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states. Common provision

(1) The law specifies what is considered to be narcotic substances, psychotropic substances, preparations containing narcotic or psychotropic substances, precursors used for the illicit manufacture of narcotic or psychotropic substances. (2) The government shall determine by regulation what is considered to be poisons within the meaning of §§ 283, 284 and 286. (3) The government shall determine by regulation which plants or mushrooms are considered to be plants and mushrooms containing narcotic or psychotropic substances under § 285. (4) The government shall determine by regulation what is considered to be substances with anabolic and other hormonal effects and what is a larger quantity within the meaning of § 288, and what is considered to be a method consisting of increasing oxygen transfer in the human body and other methods with doping effects within the meaning of § 288. Offences endangering aircraft, civilian vessels and fixed platforms

Obtaining control of aircraft, civilian vessels and fixed platforms

(1) Whoever on board an aircraft, civilian vessel or on a fixed platform on the continental shelf with the intent to obtain or exercise control over such aircraft, civilian vessel or fixed platform,

a) uses violence or threat of imminent violence against another,

b) threatens another with killing, causing bodily harm or causing large-scale damage, or

c) takes advantage of another's defencelessness, shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years or by forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence, possibly in addition to these sentences also by forfeiture of property, if

a) he causes grievous bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Endangering the safety of aircraft and civilian vessels

Whoever communicates false information that may endanger the safety or operation of an aircraft in flight or a civilian vessel at sea, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. Hijacking of aircraft to foreign territory

(1) Whoever for the purpose of hijacking an aircraft to foreign territory takes possession of such aircraft or unlawfully uses such aircraft entrusted to him, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, if he causes grievous bodily harm by the act specified in paragraph 1. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence, possibly in addition to these sentences also by forfeiture of property, if

a) he causes grievous bodily harm to at least two persons or death by the act specified in paragraph 1, or

b) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. OFFENCES AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT

Damage to and endangering of the environment

(1) Whoever in violation of another legal regulation intentionally damages or endangers soil, water, air or another component of the environment, on a larger scale or over a larger area, or in such a manner that it may cause grievous bodily harm or death or if costs on a considerable scale are required to eliminate the consequences of such conduct, or whoever intentionally increases such damage to or endangering of a component of the environment or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 repeatedly,

b) he commits such an act by breaching an important duty arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law,

c) he causes permanent or long-term damage to a component of the environment by such an act,

d) costs on a large scale are required to eliminate the consequences of such an act, or

e) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits such an act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another. Damage to and endangering of the environment by negligence

(1) Whoever in violation of another legal regulation through gross negligence damages or endangers soil, water, air or another component of the environment, on a larger scale or over a larger area, or in such a manner that it may cause grievous bodily harm or death or if costs on a considerable scale are required to eliminate the consequences of such conduct, or whoever through gross negligence increases such damage to or endangering of a component of the environment or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 by breaching an important duty arising from his employment, profession, position or function or imposed on him by law,

b) he causes permanent or long-term damage to a component of the environment by such an act, or

c) costs on a large scale are required to eliminate the consequences of such an act. Damage to water source

Whoever, even through gross negligence, causes damage to a water source for which a protection zone has been established in such a way that the reason for special protection of the water source ceases to exist or is considerably weakened, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years. Damage to forest

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, by logging forest stands or other activity carried out in violation of another legal regulation causes, even by joining an already existing bare area, the creation of a clear-cut or causes serious damage to forest over a total larger forest area or thins a forest stand below the stocking level specified by another legal regulation over a total larger forest area, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years or by prohibition of activity if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 repeatedly, or

b) a clear-cut or thinning over a total considerable forest area is created by the logging or other activity specified in paragraph 1. Common provision

(1) A larger area under §§ 293 and 294 means an area of at least three hectares and in the case of surface waters in water bodies, with the exception of watercourses, at least one hectare of water surface and for a watercourse at least two kilometres of its length; individual affected areas and lengths of watercourse are added together. (2) A total larger forest area under § 295 means an area greater than one and a half hectares and a total considerable forest area means an area greater than three hectares. (3) Damage to or endangering of soil, water, air or another component of the environment also includes operating a facility in which a hazardous activity is carried out or in which hazardous substances or mixtures are stored or used, without permission under another legal regulation. Unauthorized discharge of polluting substances

(1) Whoever in violation of an international treaty, even through negligence, discharges or, although required to do so, fails to prevent the discharge of oil, toxic liquid or other similar polluting substance from a ship or other seagoing vessel, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organized group, or

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he causes by the act specified in paragraph 1

b) serious and extensive damage to water quality, animal or plant species or parts thereof, or

c) damage to the environment, to eliminate which costs on a large scale are required. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he causes death by the act specified in paragraph 1. Unauthorized handling of waste

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, violates another legal regulation governing waste management by transporting waste across state borders without notification to or consent of the competent public authority, or in such notification or application for consent or in the attached documents states false or grossly distorted facts or conceals material facts, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever, even through negligence, in violation of another legal regulation deposits or discards, transports or otherwise handles waste, thereby causing damage to or endangering of the environment, to eliminate which costs on a considerable scale are required, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years or by prohibition of activity if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) he obtains considerable benefit for himself or another by such an act, or

c) he commits such an act repeatedly. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by a fine if

a) he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2, or

b) such an act concerns hazardous waste. Unauthorized manufacture and other handling of ozone-depleting substances

(1) Whoever in violation of another legal regulation, even through gross negligence, manufactures, imports, exports, places on the market an ozone-depleting substance or otherwise handles it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 repeatedly,

b) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another, or

c) he commits such an act on a considerable scale. Unauthorized handling of protected wild-living animals and wild-growing plants

(1) Whoever in violation of another legal regulation kills, destroys, damages, removes from nature, processes, imports, exports, transports through, stores, offers, brokers, procures for himself or another an individual of a specially protected species of animal or plant or a specimen of a protected species and commits such an act on more than twenty-five pieces of animals, plants or specimens, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever in violation of another legal regulation kills, destroys, damages, removes from nature, processes, imports, exports, transports through, stores, offers, brokers, procures for himself or another an individual of a critically or severely endangered species of animal or plant or a specimen of a species directly threatened with extinction. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another, or

c) he causes long-term or irreversible damage to a population of wild-living animals or wild-growing plants or to a local population or biotope of a specially protected species of animal or plant by such an act. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states,

b) he commits such an act with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another, or

c) he causes long-term or irreversible damage to a local population or biotope of a critically endangered species of animal or plant by such an act. Unauthorized handling of protected wild-living animals and wild-growing plants by negligence

Whoever through gross negligence violates another legal regulation by killing, destroying, damaging, removing from nature, processing, repeatedly importing, exporting or transporting through, storing, offering, brokering or procuring for himself or another an individual of a specially protected species of animal or plant or a specimen of a protected species on a larger scale than twenty-five pieces or an individual of a critically endangered species of animal or plant or a specimen of a species directly threatened with extinction, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. Damage to protected parts of nature

Whoever, even through gross negligence, violates another legal regulation by damaging or destroying a memorial tree, significant landscape element, cave, specially protected area, site of European importance or bird area in such a way that the reason for protection of such part of nature ceases to exist or is considerably weakened, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. Animal cruelty

(1) Whoever tortures an animal in a cruel or tormenting manner, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by prohibition of activity if

a) he commits such an act publicly or in a place accessible to the public,

b) he commits such an act as a member of an organized group, or

c) he continues committing such an act for an extended period. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 on a larger number of animals,

b) he causes permanent health consequences or death to the tortured animal by such an act,

c) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 in a particularly cruel or tormenting manner, or

d) he commits such an act repeatedly. Keeping animals in unsuitable conditions

(1) Whoever keeps a larger number of animals in unsuitable conditions and thereby endangers their lives or causes them considerable suffering, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) Whoever keeps animals in unsuitable conditions for the purpose of trade, or whoever profits from such keeping, and thereby endangers their lives or causes them considerable suffering, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to four years or by prohibition of activity. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 2 with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another,

b) he causes permanent consequences or death to an animal by the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2, or

c) he commits such an act as a member of an organized group. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 2 with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another,

b) he causes permanent consequences or death to a larger number of animals by the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2, or

c) he commits such an act in connection with an organized group operating in multiple states. Neglect of care for an animal by negligence

(1) Whoever through gross negligence neglects necessary care for an animal he owns or is required to care for on other grounds, and thereby causes permanent health consequences or death to it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he causes death or permanent health consequences to a larger number of animals by the act specified in paragraph 1. Poaching

(1) Whoever unlawfully hunts game or fish of not insignificant value or hides, transfers to himself or another or stores unlawfully hunted game or fish of not insignificant value, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by a fine or by forfeiture of thing if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) he obtains greater benefit for himself or another by such an act,

c) he commits such an act as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the environment,

d) he commits such an act in a particularly reprehensible manner, by mass-effective means or during the closed season, or

e) he was convicted or punished for such an act in the last three years. Unauthorized manufacture, possession and other handling of medicinal products and other substances affecting the productivity of farm animals

(1) Whoever unlawfully manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, offers, brokers, sells or otherwise procures or stores a substance with thyreostatic, gestagenic, androgenic, estrogenic or other hormonal effects, beta-agonists or another substance intended to stimulate the productivity of farm animals or a preparation containing such a substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever in violation of another legal regulation uses medicinal products for the purpose of increasing the productivity of farm animals or for that purpose manufactures, imports, exports, transports through, offers, brokers, sells or otherwise procures or stores them. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a member of an organized group,

c) with the intent to obtain considerable benefit for himself or another, or

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a member of an organized group operating in multiple states,

b) with the intent to obtain large-scale benefit for himself or another, or

Spreading contagious disease of animals

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, causes or increases the danger of introduction or spread of a contagious disease of animals in hobby farms, farm animals or wild-living animals, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes the spread of such disease by the act specified in paragraph 1. Spreading contagious disease and pest of crop plants

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, causes or increases the danger of introduction or spread of a contagious disease or pest of crop plants, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of thing. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he causes the spread of such disease or such pest by the act specified in paragraph 1. Common provision

The government shall determine by regulation which diseases of animals and plants are considered contagious within the meaning of §§ 306 and 307 and to which pests the provision of § 307 applies. OFFENCES AGAINST THE CZECH REPUBLIC, FOREIGN STATES AND INTERNATIONAL ORGANIZATIONS

Offences against the foundations of the Czech Republic, foreign states and international organizations

High treason

(1) A citizen of the Czech Republic who in connection with a foreign power or foreign agent commits the offence of subversion of the republic (§ 310), terrorist attack (§ 311), terror (§ 312) or sabotage (§ 314), shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence. Subversion of the republic

(1) Whoever with the intent to subvert the constitutional system, territorial integrity or defence capability of the Czech Republic or to destroy its independence participates in violent actions against the Czech Republic or its authorities, shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) he causes grievous bodily harm to at least two persons or death by such an act,

c) he causes large-scale damage by such an act,

d) he causes serious endangering of the international standing of the Czech Republic by such an act, or

e) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Terrorist attack

(1) Whoever with the intent to damage the constitutional system or defence capability of the Czech Republic, to disrupt or destroy the basic political, economic or social structure of the Czech Republic or an international organization, to seriously intimidate the population or to unlawfully compel a government or other public authority or international organization to do, omit or tolerate something,

a) destroys or damages to a greater extent public space, property or public facility, transport or telecommunications system, fixed platform on the continental shelf, energy, water supply, health or other important facility, including a computer system on which such facility, system or platform depends, with the aim of exposing property to the risk of large-scale damage,

b) disrupts or interrupts the supply of water, electricity or other basic natural resource with the aim of exposing property to the risk of large-scale damage,

c) seizes an aircraft, ship, other means of personal or freight transport or fixed platform on the continental shelf or exercises control over such means of transport or fixed platform or destroys or seriously damages navigational equipment or interferes to a greater extent with its operation or communicates important false information, thereby exposing property to the risk of large-scale damage,

d) exposes the property of another to the risk of large-scale damage by causing a fire or flood or harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or commits another similar dangerous act, or increases such danger or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, or

e) by inserting or transferring data into a computer system or onto an information carrier, making another intervention in the program or technical equipment of a computer system or other technical device for data processing or by deleting or otherwise destroying, damaging, altering or suppressing data stored in a computer system or on an information carrier, reducing their quality or making them unusable, carries out an attack against a computer system whose disruption would have a serious impact on the functioning of the state, health of persons, security, economy or provision of basic living needs of the population, an attack with impact on a larger number of computer systems using a computer program created or adapted for such an attack or an attack by which he causes considerable damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property. (2) Whoever with the intent to damage the constitutional system or defence capability of the Czech Republic, to disrupt or destroy the basic political, economic or social structure of the Czech Republic or an international organization, to seriously intimidate the population or to unlawfully compel a government or other public authority or international organization to do, omit or tolerate something,

a) carries out an attack endangering human life or health with the aim of causing death or grievous bodily harm,

b) seizes a hostage or carries out a kidnapping,

c) destroys or damages to a greater extent public space, property or public facility, transport or telecommunications system, fixed platform on the continental shelf, energy, water supply, health or other important facility, including a computer system on which such facility, system or platform depends, with the aim of endangering human lives or the security of such space, facility, system or platform,

d) disrupts or interrupts the supply of water, electricity or other basic natural resource with the aim of endangering human lives,

e) seizes an aircraft, ship, other means of personal or freight transport or fixed platform on the continental shelf or exercises control over such means of transport or fixed platform or destroys or seriously damages navigational equipment or interferes to a greater extent with its operation or communicates important false information, thereby endangering the life or health of people or the security of such means of transport,

f) manufactures or otherwise acquires, stores, imports, transports, exports or otherwise supplies or uses explosives, nuclear material, nuclear, biological, chemical or other weapon, combat means or material of similar nature, or conducts research and development of nuclear, biological, chemical or other weapon or combat means or explosives, or

g) exposes people to general danger of death or grievous bodily harm by causing a fire or flood or harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or commits another similar dangerous act, or increases such general danger or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, if

a) he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) he causes grievous bodily harm or death by such an act,

c) he causes a larger number of people to be left without shelter by such an act,

d) he causes large-scale damage by such an act,

e) he obtains large-scale benefit for himself or another by such an act,

f) he seriously endangers the international standing of the Czech Republic or the standing of an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member by such an act, or

g) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war. Terror

(1) Whoever with the intent to damage the constitutional system of the Czech Republic intentionally kills another, shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence. Participation in a terrorist group

(1) Whoever establishes a terrorist group or participates in the activity of a terrorist group, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, possibly in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property.(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) as a leading official or representative of a terrorist group, or

b) as a founder or member of a terrorist group that meets the characteristics of an organized criminal group.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) as a leading official or representative of a terrorist group that meets the characteristics of an organized criminal group, or

b) during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

(4) The provisions of §§ 107 and 108 shall not apply to the offender of the act referred to in paragraphs 1 to 3.

Special Provision on Effective Remorse

A person who commits the act referred to in § 312a shall not be punishable if he has made a notification about the terrorist group to a public prosecutor or police authority at a time when the danger that arose to an interest protected by this Act from another act of the terrorist group other than that referred to in § 312a could still be removed. A soldier may also make such notification to a superior.

Impunity of an Agent

(1) A police officer performing tasks as an agent pursuant to another legal regulation (hereinafter "agent"), who participates in the activities of a terrorist group, shall not be punishable for the criminal offense of participation in a terrorist group pursuant to § 312a if he committed such an act with the aim of uncovering the perpetrator of criminal activity committed by members of the terrorist group, in connection with the terrorist group or in favor of the terrorist group or to prevent its commission.

(2) An agent who participates in the activities of a terrorist group or supports such a group shall not be punishable for the criminal offense of affray pursuant to § 158 para. 1, human trafficking (§ 168), entrusting a child to the power of another (§ 169), violation of the freedom of the home (§ 178), damage to the rights of others (§ 181), violation of the secrecy of transmitted messages (§ 182), procuring (§ 189), dissemination of pornography (§ 191), production and other handling of child pornography (§ 192), abuse of a child for the production of pornography (§ 193), theft (§ 205), embezzlement (§ 206), unauthorized use of another's property (§ 207), operating dishonest games and bets (§ 213), legalization of proceeds from criminal activity (§ 216), machinations in insolvency proceedings (§ 226), violation of the obligation to make a truthful declaration of assets (§ 227), damage to another's property (§ 228), counterfeiting and alteration of money (§ 233), unauthorized procurement, counterfeiting and alteration of a means of payment (§ 234), uttering counterfeit and altered money (§ 235), production and possession of counterfeiting tools and equipment intended for unauthorized acquisition of a means of payment (§ 236), unauthorized production of money (§ 237), violation of regulations on labels and other items for marking goods (§ 244), counterfeiting and alteration of items for marking goods for tax purposes and items proving fulfillment of a fee obligation (§ 245), violation of prohibitions during a state of emergency in foreign exchange management (§ 247), unauthorized business activity (§ 251), unauthorized operation of gambling (§ 252), obtaining an advantage in the award of a public contract, in a public competition and public auction (§ 256), machinations in the award of a public contract and in a public competition (§ 257), machinations in a public auction (§ 258), violation of regulations on the circulation of goods in dealings with foreign countries (§ 261), violation of regulations on control of exports of dual-use goods and technologies (§ 262), violation of obligations in the export of dual-use goods and technologies (§ 263), distortion of data and failure to keep records regarding exports of dual-use goods and technologies (§ 264), conducting foreign trade in military material without a permit or license (§ 265), violation of obligations in connection with the issuance of a permit and license for foreign trade in military material (§ 266), distortion of data and failure to keep records regarding foreign trade in military material (§ 267), damage and endangerment of the operation of a public utility (§ 276), unlawful armament (§ 279), development, production and possession of prohibited means of warfare (§ 280), unlawful production and possession of radioactive substance and highly hazardous substance (§ 281), unlawful production and possession of nuclear material and special fissile material (§ 282), unlawful production and other handling of narcotic and psychotropic substances and poisons (§ 283), possession of narcotic and psychotropic substance and poison (§ 284), unlawful cultivation of plants containing a narcotic or psychotropic substance (§ 285), production and possession of an item for unlawful production of narcotic and psychotropic substance and poison (§ 286), production and other handling of substances with hormonal effect (§ 288), unauthorized handling of waste (§ 298), unauthorized handling of protected wild-living animals and wild-growing plants (§ 299), financing of terrorism (§ 312d), support and promotion of terrorism (§ 312e), threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f), abuse of authority of an official person (§ 329), acceptance of a bribe (§ 331), bribery (§ 332), indirect bribery pursuant to § 333 para. 2, organizing and enabling unlawful crossing of the state border (§ 340), aiding unauthorized stay on the territory of the Republic (§ 341), counterfeiting and alteration of a public document (§ 348), dangerous threatening (§ 353), defamation of a nation, race, ethnic or other group (§ 355), incitement to hatred against a group of persons or to restriction of their rights and freedoms (§ 356) or disorderly conduct (§ 358), if he committed such an act with the aim of uncovering the perpetrator of criminal activity committed by members of the terrorist group, in connection with the terrorist group or in favor of the terrorist group or to prevent its commission.

(3) An agent who founded or organized a terrorist group, or an agent who is a leading official or representative of a terrorist group, shall not become exempt from punishment.

Financing of Terrorism

(1) A person who, himself or through another, financially or materially supports a terrorist group, its member, a terrorist or the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, a criminal offense of support and promotion of terrorism (§ 312e) or threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f) or collects financial resources or other items with the intent that they be used in such manner, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organized group,

b) during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, or

Support and Promotion of Terrorism

(1) A person who publicly incites the commission of a terrorist criminal offense, or who publicly approves a committed terrorist criminal offense or publicly praises its perpetrator, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years.

(2) The following shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property:

a) a person who recruits another to commit a terrorist criminal offense or a criminal offense of participation in a terrorist group (§ 312a),

b) a person who provides another with information or training concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purposes of committing a terrorist criminal offense,

c) a person who obtains information or acquires skills concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purpose of committing a terrorist criminal offense, or

d) a person who, himself or through another, provides or promises a reward or compensation to another or for another for the commission of a terrorist criminal offense or organizes a collection for such reward or compensation.

(3) The following shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 2:

a) a person who travels to another state or to the Czech Republic for the purpose of committing a terrorist criminal offense, a criminal offense of participation in a terrorist group (§ 312a), threatening with a terrorist criminal offense (§ 312f) or a criminal offense referred to in paragraph 2 letter b) or c), or

b) a person who organizes such travel for another, induces another to do so or enables or facilitates such travel for another.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 letter b) or d) by press, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means,

b) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 as a member of an organized group,

c) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, or

d) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 2 letter a) or b) against a child.

Threatening with a Terrorist Criminal Offense

(1) A person who threatens the commission of a terrorist criminal offense shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years, or in addition to this sentence by forfeiture of property, if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organized group,

b) by press, film, radio, television, publicly accessible computer network or other similarly effective means,

d) during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

Common Provision

Protection pursuant to §§ 311 and 312 shall also be provided to a foreign state.

Sabotage

(1) A person who, with the intent to damage the constitutional system or defense capability of the Czech Republic or to damage an international organization, abuses his employment, occupation, position or function or commits other conduct in order to

a) frustrate or impede the fulfillment of an important task of an international organization, a public authority, armed forces or a security corps, an economic organization or other institution, or

b) cause a disruption or other serious damage in the activities of such an authority or such an organization or institution, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group, or

b) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years, or in addition to this sentence also by forfeiture of property, or by an exceptional sentence, if

a) by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes grievous bodily harm to at least two persons or death,

b) by such an act he causes damage on a large scale,

c) by such an act he seriously endangers the international standing of the Czech Republic or the standing of an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, or

d) he organizes such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

Abuse of Representation of the State and International Organization

(1) A person who, with the intent to damage the constitutional system, territorial integrity or defense capability of the Czech Republic or to destroy its independence or to damage an international organization, being authorized to represent the Czech Republic or an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, or to negotiate with a foreign power or otherwise protect the interests of the Czech Republic or an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, abuses his authority to act on behalf of the Czech Republic or the said international organization in dealings with a person representing the interests of a foreign power, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years.

(2) A person who, with such intent, pretends to be an authorized person and enters into negotiations on important matters of the Czech Republic or an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member with a person representing the interests of a foreign power shall be punished in the same manner.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to twelve years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organized group,

b) by such an act he causes serious endangerment of the international standing of the Czech Republic or the standing of an international organization of which the Czech Republic is a member, or

c) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

Criminal Offenses against the Security of the Czech Republic, a Foreign State and an International Organization

Espionage

(1) A person who seeks out information classified pursuant to another legal regulation, the misuse of which may seriously endanger or damage the constitutional system, sovereignty, territorial integrity, defense and security of the Czech Republic or another state or the defense and security of an international organization, for the protection of whose interests in the said areas the Czech Republic has committed itself, with the aim of revealing it to a foreign power, a person who, with such aim, collects data containing classified information, or a person who intentionally reveals such classified information to a foreign power, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

(2) A person who enables or facilitates the activity of the offender of the act referred to in paragraph 1 or an organization whose aim is to seek out classified information shall be punished in the same manner.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of an organization whose aim is to seek out classified information,

b) he commits such an act although the protection of classified information was specifically entrusted to him,

c) by such an act he obtains for himself or another a substantial benefit or commits such an act on a substantial scale, or

d) such an act concerns classified information classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret".

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

Endangerment of Classified Information

(1) A person who seeks out information classified pursuant to another legal regulation with the aim of revealing it to an unauthorized person, a person who, with such aim, collects data containing classified information or a person who intentionally reveals such classified information to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if

a) he intentionally reveals to an unauthorized person classified information classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret" or "Secret",

b) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 although the protection of classified information was specifically entrusted to him, or

c) by such an act he obtains for himself or another a substantial benefit or causes substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if

a) the act referred to in paragraph 1 concerns classified information in the area of ensuring the defense capability of the Czech Republic classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret", or

b) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

Endangerment of Classified Information by Negligence

A person who by negligence causes the disclosure of classified information classified in another legal regulation with the classification level "Top Secret" or "Secret" shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity.

Criminal Offenses against the Defense of the State

Cooperation with the Enemy

(1) A person who, during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, procures an advantage for the enemy or supports him in any way, shall be punished, if it is not an act subject to more severe punishment, by imprisonment for one to ten years.

War Treason

(1) A citizen of the Czech Republic who, during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war, serves in enemy armed forces shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty years or by an exceptional sentence.

Service in Foreign Armed Forces

(1) A citizen of the Czech Republic who, in violation of another legal regulation, serves in the army or armed forces of another state shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.

Participation in a Non-State Armed Group Aimed at Acting in an Armed Conflict

A person who participates in the activities of a non-state armed group aimed at acting in an armed conflict taking place on the territory of another state by

a) engaging in the combat activities of such a group,

b) providing another with information or training concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purpose of committing the act referred to in letter a),

c) obtaining information or acquiring skills concerning the production or use of explosives, weapons, hazardous substances or materials of a similar nature or other similar methods or techniques for the purpose of committing the act referred to in letter a) or b), or

d) traveling to another state or to the Czech Republic for the purpose of committing the act referred to in letter a), b) or c), shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.

Violation of Personal and Material Obligation for the Defense of the State

(1) A person who, even by negligence, fails to fulfill a personal or material obligation for the defense of the state, a person who intentionally evades the fulfillment of such an obligation, or a person who, even by negligence, frustrates or impedes the fulfillment of such an obligation by another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, he endangers the interests of the defense of the state to a substantial degree.

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER

Criminal Offenses against the Exercise of Authority of a Public Authority and an Official Person

Violence against a Public Authority

(1) A person who uses violence with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon,

b) by such an act he causes injury to another, or

c) by such an act he causes substantial damage.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to twelve years if, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, he causes

b) damage on a large scale, or

c) a disruption in the activities of such an authority.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to eighteen years if, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, he causes death.

Threatening with the Aim of Influencing a Public Authority

(1) A person who threatens another with death, injury or causing substantial damage

a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority, or

b) for the exercise of authority of such an authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon.

Violence against an Official Person

a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of an official person, or

b) for the exercise of authority of an official person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to six years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon,

b) by such an act he causes injury to another, or

c) by such an act he causes greater damage.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years if, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, he causes

a) grievous bodily harm, or

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to sixteen years if, by the act referred to in paragraph 1, he causes death.

Threatening with the Aim of Influencing an Official Person

(1) A person who threatens another with death, injury or causing substantial damage

a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of an official person, or

b) for the exercise of authority of an official person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with a weapon.

Common Provision

(1) Protection pursuant to §§ 323 to 326 shall also be provided to a person who appeared in support or in defense of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority or an official person.

(2) Protection pursuant to §§ 323 to 326 shall also be provided to an internationally protected person, which means an official person of a foreign state or international organization who enjoys diplomatic or other privileges and immunities under international law, or a person holding a function or employed or working in an international judicial body.

Usurpation of Authority of an Office

A person who unlawfully performs acts that are reserved to a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority, or a person who performs an act that can only be performed by the authority of an official body of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

Criminal Offenses of Official Persons

Abuse of Authority of an Official Person

(1) An official person who, with the intent to cause another damage or other serious detriment or to procure for himself or another an unauthorized benefit

a) exercises his authority in a manner contrary to another legal regulation,

b) exceeds his authority, or

c) fails to fulfill an obligation arising from his authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years or by prohibition of activity.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if

a) by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he procures for himself or another a substantial benefit,

b) he commits such an act against another for his actual or supposed race, membership in an ethnic group, nationality, political conviction, religion or because he is actually or supposedly without religion,

c) by such an act he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority,

d) by such an act he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a legal person or natural person who is an entrepreneur,

e) he commits such an act by abusing the defenselessness, dependence, distress, mental weakness or inexperience of another, or

f) by such an act he causes substantial damage.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years or by forfeiture of property if

a) by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he procures for himself or another a benefit on a large scale, or

b) by such an act he causes damage on a large scale.

Frustration of the Task of an Official Person by Negligence

(1) An official person who, in the exercise of his authority, by negligence frustrates or substantially impedes the fulfillment of an important task shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by prohibition of activity if

a) by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a state administration authority, territorial self-government authority, court or other public authority,

b) by such an act he causes a serious disruption in the activities of a legal person or natural person who is an entrepreneur,

c) by such an act he causes substantial damage, or

d) by such an act he secures for another a substantial benefit.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if

a) by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes damage on a large scale, or

b) by such an act he secures for another a benefit on a large scale.

Bribery

Acceptance of a Bribe

(1) A person who, himself or through another, in connection with handling matters of general interest, accepts or has promised to himself or another a bribe, or a person who, himself or through another, in connection with his or another's business, accepts or has promised to himself or another a bribe, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years or by prohibition of activity.

(2) A person who, under the circumstances referred to in paragraph 1, requests a bribe shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intent to procure for himself or another a substantial benefit, or

b) he commits such an act as an official person.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 with the intent to procure for himself or another a benefit on a large scale, or

b) he commits such an act as an official person with the intent to procure for himself or another a substantial benefit.

Bribery

(1) A person who, himself or through another, provides, offers or promises a bribe to another or for another in connection with handling matters of general interest, or a person who, himself or through another, provides, offers or promises a bribe to another or for another in connection with his or another's business, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by a fine.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years, by forfeiture of property or by a fine if

a) he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to procure for himself or another a substantial benefit or to cause another substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence, or

b) he commits such an act toward an official person.

Indirect Bribery

(1) A person who, himself or through another, requests, has promised to himself or accepts a bribe for using his influence or through another to influence the exercise of authority of an official person, or for having already done so, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) A person who, himself or through another, for the reason referred to in paragraph 1, provides, offers or promises a bribe to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

Common Provision

(1) A bribe means an unauthorized advantage consisting of direct material enrichment or other preferential treatment that is or is to be given to the bribed person or, with his consent, to another person, and to which there is no entitlement.

(2) An official person pursuant to §§ 331 to 333 means, in addition to the person referred to in § 127, also any person

a) holding a function in a legislative body, judicial body or other public authority of a foreign state,

b) holding a function or employed or working in an international judicial body,

c) holding a function or employed or working in an international or supranational organization created by states or other subjects of public international law or in its body or institution or acting on behalf of such an organization, or

d) holding a function in a business legal person in which the Czech Republic or a foreign state has decisive influence, if the exercise of such function, employment or work is connected with authority in handling matters of general interest and the criminal offense was committed in connection with this authority.

(3) Handling matters of general interest also means observing an obligation imposed by a legal regulation or contractually assumed, the purpose of which is to ensure that in business relations there is no damage to or unjustified preferential treatment of participants in these relations or persons acting on their behalf.

Other Disruptions of the Activity of a Public Authority

Interference with the Independence of a Court

(1) A person who influences a judge to violate his obligations in proceedings before a court shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to

a) procure for himself or another a substantial benefit,

b) cause substantial damage, or

c) seriously harm another in employment, disturb his family relations or cause him other serious detriment.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to

a) procure for himself or another a benefit on a large scale, or

b) cause damage on a large scale.

Contempt of Court

a) seriously disrupts court proceedings,

b) behaves offensively toward the court or disparages the court in such proceedings, or

c) without sufficient excuse disobeys a command or summons of the court or frustrates court proceedings, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by forfeiture of an item.

Frustration of the Execution of an Official Decision and Expulsion

(1) A person who frustrates or substantially impedes the execution of a decision of a court or other public authority by

a) performing an activity that was prohibited by such a decision or for which the corresponding authorization was withdrawn pursuant to another legal regulation or for which he lost such authorization,

b) entering the territory of the Czech Republic although he was imposed the punishment of expulsion or a decision was made on his administrative expulsion, or staying on its territory despite the imposed punishment of expulsion or decision on administrative expulsion after measures aimed at his departure were unsuccessfully used against him,

c) without permission and without a serious reason staying in a place or district to which the punishment of prohibition of residence applies, or not observing restrictions that were imposed on him by a court in connection with the execution of this punishment,

d) repeatedly or systematically engaging in conduct to frustrate the purpose of a restrictive measure imposed in misdemeanor proceedings, which consists of a prohibition on visiting places where sporting events take place, or to frustrate the purpose of a restrictive measure imposed in misdemeanor proceedings pursuant to the Act on Addictive Substances, which consists of a prohibition on visiting certain publicly accessible places,

e) visiting an event to which the punishment of prohibition of entry to sporting, cultural and other social events applies, or engaging in other serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of this punishment,

f) keeping or breeding an animal to which the punishment of prohibition of keeping and breeding animals applies, or engaging in other serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of this punishment,

g) without a serious reason failing to report to serve the punishment of imprisonment upon summons by the court or otherwise unlawfully resisting commencement of the execution of this punishment,

h) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of a punishment,

i) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of custody,

j) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of security detention, or

k) engaging in serious conduct to frustrate the execution or purpose of protective treatment or protective education that were imposed by a court, or otherwise, particularly by escape from an institution, assistance in escape, substantially impedes the execution of such decisions, or frustrates supervision imposed upon termination of protective treatment, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) A person who engages in serious or repeated conduct to frustrate expulsion carried out pursuant to another legal regulation or a decision on a preliminary injunction of a court imposing the obligation to temporarily leave the shared dwelling and its immediate vicinity and refrain from entering it or the obligation to refrain from contact with the applicant and initiating contacts with him shall be punished in the same manner.

(3) A person who frustrates or substantially impedes the execution of a decision of a court or other public authority by

a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, alienating or removing an item to which such a decision relates, or

b) escaping from a guard, from custody, from the execution of imprisonment or security detention, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years or by a fine.

(4) A person who, after measures in civil court proceedings aimed at the execution of a court decision or a court-approved agreement on the upbringing of minor children, including arrangements for contact with the child, were unsuccessfully used against him, frustrates the execution of such a decision or agreement, or a person who engages in serious conduct to frustrate the execution of a decision of another public authority concerning the upbringing of minor children, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

Release of a Prisoner

(1) A person who releases a prisoner or detainee in the execution of security detention, organizes his escape, induces him to escape or prepares his escape, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, by prohibition of activity or by forfeiture of an item.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

Violent Crossing of the State Border

(1) A person who crosses the state border using violence or threat of imminent violence shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years or by forfeiture of property if

a) he organizes the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) he commits such an act with a weapon or with at least two persons,

c) he commits such an act with the intent to conceal or facilitate another criminal offense,

d) by such an act he causes grievous bodily harm,

e) by such an act he causes substantial damage, or

f) he commits such an act during a state of threat to the state or during a state of war.(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years, or in addition to this punishment also by forfeiture of property,

a) if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) if he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons by such act,

c) if he causes damage on a large scale by such act, or

d) if he commits such act as a soldier during a state of emergency or during a state of war. Organizing and Enabling Unlawful Crossing of State Border

(1) Whoever organizes for another the unlawful crossing of a state border or enables or assists another to unlawfully cross a state border or enables or assists another, after unlawful crossing of a state border, to be transported across the territory of the Czech Republic or organizes such transport, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by forfeiture of property or by a pecuniary punishment,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group,

b) if he subjects another to inhuman or degrading treatment by such act,

c) if he commits such act for remuneration,

d) if he commits such act repeatedly, or

e) if he commits such act with the intention of concealing or facilitating another criminal offense. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years or by forfeiture of property,

a) if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a member of an organized group and for remuneration,

b) if he subjects a larger number of persons to inhuman or degrading treatment by such act,

c) if he puts another in danger of death by such act,

d) if he causes serious bodily harm by such act,

e) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by such act,

f) if he commits such act with a weapon, or

g) if he commits such act during a state of emergency or during a state of war. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years, or in addition to this punishment also by forfeiture of property,

a) if he causes death by the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) if he causes serious bodily harm to at least two persons by such act,

c) if he obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or another by such act, or

d) if he commits such act as a soldier during a state of emergency or during a state of war. Assisting Unauthorized Stay on the Territory of the Republic

(1) Whoever assists another in unauthorized stay on the territory of the Czech Republic with the intention of obtaining unlawful property or other benefit shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by prohibition of activity. (2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years, if

a) he organizes the act referred to in paragraph 1,

b) he commits such act as a member of an organized group,

c) he commits such act repeatedly, or

d) he commits such act with the intention of concealing or facilitating another criminal offense. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years, by forfeiture of property or by a pecuniary punishment,

a) if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act during a state of emergency or during a state of war. (4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years, or in addition to this punishment also by forfeiture of property,

a) if he obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1, or

b) if he commits such act as a soldier during a state of emergency or during a state of war. Common Provision

Protection under §§ 339 to 341 is also provided to a state that is a Member State of the European Union or applies the Schengen Border Code in full. Unauthorized Employment of Foreigners

(1) Whoever systematically, repeatedly, under particularly exploitative working conditions or on a larger scale unlawfully employs or arranges employment for a foreigner who is staying unlawfully on the territory of the Czech Republic or does not have a valid work permit, where required by another legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months, by forfeiture of a thing or by prohibition of activity. (2) The same punishment shall be imposed on anyone who employs or arranges employment for a foreigner who is a child and is staying unlawfully on the territory of the Czech Republic or does not have a valid work permit under another legal regulation. (3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year, if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a member of an organized group,

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years, if he obtains substantial benefit for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. (5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years, or in addition to this punishment also by forfeiture of property, if he obtains benefit on a large scale for himself or another by the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2. Violation of Regulations on International Flights

Whoever violates the laws on international flights by entering the territory of the Czech Republic in an aircraft shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years. Mutiny of Prisoners

(1) Whoever takes part in a revolt or resistance of a group of prisoners or persons in the execution of security detention against the supervisory body, its order, the prison rules or the rules for the execution of security detention shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.
